### [1] [Analytical Interacting Tight-binding Model for Fractional Chern Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23409)
*Lexu Zhao,Jayden S. Chang,Awwab A. Azam,Jiabin Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 构造二维双带紧束缚模型，跳迁与相互作用均以高斯形式指数局域，无需能带投影即得到精确的分数量子霍尔绝缘体基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔态（FCI）通常需要在Chern平带中通过能带投影才能获得精确基态，缺乏无需投影的严格可解晶格模型。

Method: 建立二维2带紧束缚模型，包含两体相互作用，无磁平移；跳迁与相互作用均在实空间高斯衰减指数局域，相互作用系数取跳迁幅度除以相对距离的正幂次；不作任何能带投影，在周期/扭转边界条件下分析1/3填充基态，并解析构造加入一个元胞后的一准空穴态。

Result: 1/3填充基态对任意有限晶格尺寸和任意周期/扭转边界条件均为精确FCI态（有非零能隙），类似连续Chern-ideal能带中Laughlin态的离散化版本，但不需要连续情形所必需的on-site旋量结构；加入一个元胞并固定电子数后，任意位置的任意子一准空穴态在校准哈密顿量下为零能基态，类似Haldane V1赝势下的Laughlin准空穴；数值证据表明当相互作用强度远小于单粒子能隙时，1/3填充FCI态在热力学极限下可能有非零能隙。

Conclusion: 提供了一个无需能带投影即可精确实现FCI及任意子准空穴的晶格模型，为FCI的严格解析研究提供新途径。

Abstract: We construct a two-dimensional 2-band tight-binding model with two-body interactions and without magnetic lattice translation, in which both the hopping and interaction are exponentially local (with Gaussian decay) in real space. The interaction is built from pair annihilation operators whose coefficients are hopping amplitudes divided by positive powers of relative distance. Without any band projection, the ground states at $1/3$ filling are exactly fractional Chern insulator (FCI) states (and thus have nonzero gap) for any finite lattice size with any periodic/twist boundary conditions. The FCI ground states are analogous to the discretized version of the Laughlin-like states in the continuum Chern-ideal band, though our FCI states do not require the onsite spinor structure that is necessary in the continuum. We further show analytically that, after adding one unit cell while keeping electron number fixed, the anyonic one-quasihole states are zero-energy ground states of the full Hamiltonian for any finite system size and any quasihole position, analogous to Laughlin quasiholes under the Haldane $V_1$ pseudopotential. Numerical evidence is provided for the possible nonzero gap of the FCI states at $1/3$ filling in the thermodynamic limit, when interaction strength is much smaller than the single-particle band gap.

</details>


### [2] [Synthesis-route-dependent state selection in the frustrated spinel ZnV$_2$O$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23500)
*Jem Pitcairn,Jennifer N. Graham,Jacob L. Brownlee,Shruti Chakravarty,Joya A. Cooley,Giorgia Confalonieri,A. Dominic Fortes,David W. Tam,J. Ross Stewart,Lucy Clark*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文以尖晶石 ZnV2O4 为模型体系，证明固相合成路径可作为调控强关联材料基态的可控实验变量：微波辅助固相合成（MASS）将立方尖晶石结构稳定至低温，抑制常规立方—四方相变，保留高度阻挫的 S=1 烧绿石晶格，得到兼具持续类自旋液体动力学与部分自旋冻结的磁性基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联材料的涌现性质对制备方式高度敏感，但合成路径极少被视为决定基态的基本变量。ZnV2O4 中 V3+（S=1）烧绿石晶格存在竞争性的自旋与轨道简并，且不同合成路线给出的低温行为差异显著，因而是检验该设想的理想模型体系。

Method: 对比不同固相合成路径，重点采用微波辅助固相合成（MASS）制备样品；结合高分辨衍射、磁测量与非弹性中子散射对结构与磁性基态进行表征。

Result: MASS 样品中立方尖晶石结构可稳定至低温，常规立方—四方结构相变被抑制，阻挫 S=1 烧绿石晶格得以保持；磁性基态同时表现出持续的类自旋液体动力学与部分自旋冻结，且该结果可重复获得。

Conclusion: 合成路径可作为在竞争性涌现态之间进行选择的实验变量，为获取常规合成无法实现的强关联材料基态提供了新途径。

Abstract: The emergent properties of strongly correlated materials can be highly sensitive to how they are made, yet synthesis pathway is rarely treated as a fundamental variable governing their ground states. The spinel ZnV$_2$O$_4$ provides a model system in which to test this idea: its pyrochlore lattice of antiferromagnetically coupled V$^{3+}$ $S=1$ moments hosts competing spin and orbital degeneracies, while different synthesis routes yield markedly different low-temperature behaviour. Here, we show that changing the solid-state synthesis pathway reproducibly selects a distinct structural and magnetic ground state. Microwave-assisted solid-state synthesis (MASS) stabilises the cubic spinel structure to low temperature, suppressing the conventional cubic-to-tetragonal transition and preserving the highly frustrated $S=1$ pyrochlore lattice. Combining high-resolution diffraction, magnetometry and inelastic neutron scattering, we find a magnetic ground state exhibiting persistent spin-liquid-like dynamics together with partial spin freezing. Our results establish synthesis pathway as an experimental variable for selecting between competing emergent states in strongly correlated materials, revealing a route to accessing ground states not realised by conventional synthesis.

</details>


### [3] [Implementation of LDA+Gutzwiller with Newton's method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23842)
*Jian Zhang,Ming-Feng Tian,Guang-Xi Jin,Yuan-Feng Xu,Xi Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出用牛顿法实现LDA+Gutzwiller方法，显著提升自洽计算效率与收敛性，并在典型强关联材料上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算关联材料电子结构时，原LDA+Gutzwiller方法采用线性混合(linear mix)自洽迭代，效率与收敛性不足，需改进。

Method: 在LDA+Gutzwiller框架中引入牛顿法进行自洽求解，并结合黄金分割搜索(golden section search)及其他改进策略；与原有线性混合法作对比。

Result: 牛顿法使自洽过程、计算效率与收敛性均大幅改善；应用于SrVO3、LaCoO3、La2O3Fe2Se2等强关联材料，结果与已有研究吻合良好。

Conclusion: 基于牛顿法的LDA+Gutzwiller实现可行且高效，可作为研究强关联材料电子结构的可靠工具。

Abstract: In order to calculate the electronic structure of correlated materials, we propose implementation of the LDA+Gutzwiller method with Newton's method. The self-consistence process, efficiency and convergence of calculation are improved dramatically by using Newton's method with golden section search and other improvement approaches. We compare the calculated results by applying the previous linear mix method and Newton's method. We have applied our code to study the electronic structure of several typical strong correlated materials, including SrVO$_3$, LaCoO$_3$, and La$_2$O$_3$Fe$_2$Se$_2$. Our results fit quite well with the previous studies.

</details>


### [4] [Local spin constraints and symmetry in single-$Q$ spiral magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23882)
*Lasin Thaivalappil,Shin-Ming Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出满足局域自旋长度约束的 LT 改进框架：单子格化为久期方程，双子格通过消去相对相位得到框架无关能量；数值表明约束可降低能量并改变优选序波矢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Luttinger–Tisza (LT) 方法虽能给出经典自旋模型能量的下界，但它放松了局域自旋长度约束；对一般不可公度单-Q螺旋，固定自旋长度要求每个磁子格上都是圆极化，而最低 LT 本征模中子格权重相等并不能保证这一条件，因此需要发展显式满足局域约束的处理框架。

Method: 针对含一个或两个磁子格的一般双线性经典自旋哈密顿量，构建受约束的变分表述：单子格情形下，最小化化为关于螺旋面法向的久期方程；双子格情形下，通过精确消去相对相位，在两个单位法向构成的空间上得到与参考框架无关的能量泛函。同时，通过区分保持/反转傅里叶臂的对称操作以及保持/交换子格的对称操作，导出体系的对称性约束。

Result: 对具有对称各向异性的模型，在两个螺旋面法向上进行数值优化所得能量低于两种受约束的纯交换扇区态，尽管线性哈密顿量可块对角化；另一个各向异性双子格基准算例表明，即使最低 LT 本征模具有相等子格权重，强制局域圆极化也会移动优选的序波矢。

Conclusion: 最低 LT 本征模的等权子格结构不足以判定真实基态：显式施加局域自旋长度（圆极化）约束不仅可降低能量，还能改变优选序波矢，说明受约束表述对经典螺旋磁序的判定是必要且更准确的。

Abstract: The Luttinger--Tisza (LT) method provides a lower bound on the energy of classical spin models but relaxes the local spin-length constraints. For a generic incommensurate single-$Q$ spiral, fixed spin length requires circular polarization on every magnetic sublattice, a condition not guaranteed by equal sublattice weights in the lowest LT eigenmode. We develop a constrained formulation for general bilinear classical spin Hamiltonians with one or two magnetic sublattices. For one sublattice, minimization reduces to a secular equation for the spiral-plane normal. For two sublattices, exact elimination of the relative phase gives a frame-independent energy on the space of two unit normals. We derive symmetry constraints by distinguishing operations that preserve or reverse the Fourier arm and those that preserve or exchange sublattices. For a model with symmetric anisotropy, numerical optimization over both spiral-plane normals yields a lower energy than either constrained pure exchange-sector state, despite the block diagonalization of the linear Hamiltonian. A separate anisotropic two-sublattice benchmark demonstrates that enforcing local circularity can shift the preferred ordering wave vector even when the lowest LT eigenmode has equal sublattice weights.

</details>


### [5] [Molecular Kondo Ball Based on an Endohedral Fullerene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23918)
*Xiangyang Li,Xiao Zheng,Bing Wang,Jinlong Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文理论预测了TiSc₂N@C₈₀/Al(111)体系中的"分子Kondo球"：Ti局域自旋通过富勒烯笼内部类巡游电子库实现Kondo屏蔽，Kondo温度约60 K，且对吸附取向稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子Kondo体系通常依赖分子与衬底的直接杂化，屏蔽通道受吸附构型与分子取向强烈影响。本文旨在提出一种由闭合碳笼内部作为电子库的新型屏蔽架构，实现取向容错的Kondo态。

Method: 第一性原理计算（DFT）分析TiSc₂N@C₈₀在Al(111)等衬底上的电子结构与杂化；采用层级运动方程（HEOM）模拟温度依赖的Fano共振与Kondo屏蔽。

Result: 中间程度的分子–衬底杂化将离散的C₈₀分子能级展宽为类巡游态，同时保留Ti的S=1/2局域磁矩；HEOM给出温度依赖Fano线型，Kondo温度约60 K；Cd(0001)、In(101)衬底具有相似电子条件；关键电子特征对吸附取向不敏感。

Conclusion: 闭合富勒烯笼可作为分子内部类巡游电子库，构建"分子Kondo球"，提供取向容错的Kondo屏蔽架构，为分子自旋电子学中鲁棒的Kondo态设计提供新思路。

Abstract: We theoretically predict a molecular Kondo ball in TiSc$_2$N@C$_{80}$/Al(111), where a Ti-centered $S = 1/2$ local moment is encapsulated within a closed fullerene $π$ cage that serves as an itinerant-like electronic reservoir for Kondo screening. First-principles calculations show that intermediate molecule--substrate hybridization broadens the discrete C$_{80}$ molecular levels into itinerant-like electronic states while preserving the Ti local moment, with similar electronic conditions found for Cd(0001) and In(101).Hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) simulations reveal a temperature-dependent Fano resonance with a Kondo temperature of about 60 K.The key electronic characteristics underlying the Kondo state remain robust against adsorption orientation, demonstrating that the closed cage geometry provides an orientation-tolerant molecular architecture for Kondo screening.

</details>


### [6] [Stabilizer models of achiral abelian topological orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24105)
*Marvin Qi,Wilbur Shirley*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文构造连续变量稳定子码，实现了所有非手性二维阿贝尔拓扑序（玻色型与费米型），方法是在 R^N 规范理论中对平凡的成对互编织双子晶格进行凝聚，并通过规范化 R^N toric code 的非格点 1-形式对称性在微观上实现该凝聚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有稳定子码构造主要针对特定拓扑序，难以统一覆盖任意 signature-zero 整数 K-矩阵所对应的全部非手性二维阿贝尔拓扑序，缺少系统的构造框架。

Method: 以任意 signature-zero 整数 K-矩阵为起点，在 R^N 规范理论中确定成对互编织平凡的双子（dyon）晶格，对其凝聚；再通过规范化 R^N toric code 的非格点 1-形式对称性，将这一凝聚过程在微观层面实现为连续变量稳定子码。

Result: 给出了一个统一构造，可同时得到玻色型与费米型的全部非手性二维阿贝尔拓扑序，且以连续变量稳定子码形式显式实现。

Conclusion: 非格点 1-形式对称性的规范化提供了实现任意 signature-zero K-矩阵拓扑序的通用机制，从而将阿贝尔拓扑序的分类数据与显式量子纠错码构造对应起来。

Abstract: We construct continuous-variable stabilizer codes realizing every achiral 2D abelian topological order, both bosonic and fermionic. Starting from an arbitrary signature-zero integer $K$-matrix, we show that the corresponding topological order can be obtained by condensing a lattice of dyons with trivial pairwise mutual braiding in an $\mathbb{R}^N$ gauge theory. We implement this condensation microscopically by gauging a non-on-site 1-form symmetry of the $\mathbb{R}^N$ toric code.

</details>


### [7] [Hidden topological signatures in a van der Waals ferromagnet revealed by spin-resolved circular-dichroic photoemission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24468)
*Jay R. Paudel,Federico Mazzola,Lidia A. Lapinski,Barun Ghosh,Valeria Rosa Rocha,Abigail M. Derrico,Nikola Maksimovic,Iulia Cojocariu,Daniel Baranowski,Christoph Klewe,Vitaliy Feyer,Ivana Vobornik,Claus M. Schneider,Giancarlo Panaccione,Arun Bansil,James G. Analytis,Alexander X. Gray*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合自旋分辨/角分辨光电子能谱、圆二色性测量与DFT计算，揭示Ni₁/₄TaSe₂铁磁态中自旋极化能带、时间反演对称性破缺及螺旋度依赖自旋响应所暗示的自旋贝里曲率与局域拓扑特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状强关联铁磁体是研究磁性、自旋轨道耦合与非平庸能带拓扑相互作用的平台；Ni₁/₄TaSe₂（Tc=58 K）成分上接近Ni贫化后出现超导的相区，其自旋分辨电子结构及拓扑性质尚待实验确认，并可能与超导起源相关。

Method: 综合自旋分辨与角分辨光电子能谱（spin-ARPES）、圆二色性（CD）测量以及密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，对Ni₁/₄TaSe₂的自旋分辨电子结构进行表征。

Result: 观测到穿过费米能级的自旋极化态；在铁磁态中观察到时间反演对称性破缺的特征；自旋分辨圆二色性测量显示与有限自旋贝里曲率一致、暗示局域拓扑性的螺旋度依赖自旋响应。

Conclusion: 实验结果在Ni₁/₄TaSe₂中建立了铁磁性、自旋极化电子态与拓扑特征之间的实验联系，为研究这些性质如何与Ni贫化诱导的超导出现相关联奠定基础。

Abstract: Layered ferromagnets with strongly correlated electrons provide a platform for exploring the interplay among magnetism, spin-orbit coupling, and nontrivial band topology. $\mathrm{Ni}_{1/4}\mathrm{TaSe}_2$ is a correlated van der Waals ferromagnet with a transition temperature of 58 K that lies close in composition to a superconducting phase that emerges upon Ni depletion. Here, we combine spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, circular-dichroism measurements, and density functional theory calculations to investigate its spin-resolved electronic structure. We observe spin-polarized states crossing the Fermi level and signatures of broken time-reversal symmetry in the ferromagnetic state. Spin-resolved circular-dichroism measurements further reveal a helicity-dependent spin response consistent with a finite spin Berry curvature and suggestive of local topological character. Together, these results establish an experimental connection between ferromagnetism, spin-polarized electronic states, and topological signatures in $\mathrm{Ni}_{1/4}\mathrm{TaSe}_2$, providing a foundation for investigating how these properties may relate to the emergence of superconductivity upon Ni depletion.

</details>


### [8] [From geometric curvature to effective backgrounds: a dictionary for Dirac fermions in one, two and three spatial dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24878)
*David Valenzuela,Alfredo Raya*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 从单一Clifford代数恒等式出发，将弯曲背景上的Dirac方程改写为平直空间方程，其中几何以张量型Fermi速度与一组背景场的形式出现，并在一至三维中系统给出两类收缩项的结构、变换律与物理应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弯曲背景上的Dirac方程中，自旋联络与Fermi速度的空间依赖如何被完全吸收进平直空间的等效背景场（矢量势、赝标量质量、轴矢量势），此前缺乏统一且维度一般的推导；同时需要厘清这些纯几何项与应变样品中占主导的晶格赝规范场之间的区别。

Method: 以单一Clifford代数恒等式为出发点构造对应关系；用Ricci旋转系数表示自旋联络，证明只可能出现两种收缩：(1)迹收缩，在所有维度都给出矢量势型指标结构，并带虚因子i，恰好保证位置依赖Fermi速度下算符的厄米性；(2)全反对称收缩，其形式依赖Levi-Civita符号可用指标数——线上为迹、面上为赝标量质量、体上为轴矢量势。给出两类项在局域标架旋转下的变换律（均非不变）。随后在Gaussian凸起、量子线、曲面定常标架、以及扭转与旋转标架等情形中逐项求解。

Result: 曲面上迹项的旋度总可通过标架旋转消去，而其散度正比于高斯曲率。应用中：Gaussian凸起中心处局域态密度低于平直值，并在低能下有闭合表达式；非均匀lapse可在能隙内束缚态而非均匀速度不能；曲面定常标架下环流拖曳使Dirac质量局域移动且两种符号皆可出现；体中扭转标架的轴项表现为纯由标架产生的 chiral 化学势，旋转标架则使Weyl节点分离。最后将几何贡献与主导应变样品响应的晶格赝规范场区分开。

Conclusion: 弯曲几何对Dirac费米子的效应可完全归结为平直空间中的张量型Fermi速度加少量背景场，且由Clifford恒等式两点收缩穷尽；其中虚矢量势项是厄米性的保证，轴项则随维度改变性质。几何项在高斯曲率、局域态密度、低维束缚与手性化学势上有可观测后果，但相比晶格赝规范场通常是次主导贡献。

Abstract: Dirac equation on curved backgrounds can be rewritten as a flat-space equation in which geometry appears as a tensorial Fermi velocity together with a set of background fields. We derive this correspondence from a single Clifford-algebra identity and follow it through one, two, and three spatial dimensions. Once the spin connection is written in terms of Ricci rotation coefficients, only two contractions can occur. The trace produces a term with the index structure of a vector potential in every dimensionality; it enters with an imaginary factor i and is precisely the term that keeps the operator Hermitian when the Fermi velocity depends on position. The totally antisymmetric contraction produces an object that depends on the number of indices available to the Levi-Civita symbol: a trace on a line, a pseudoscalar mass on a surface, and an axial vector potential in a volume. Neither contraction is invariant under local rotations of the frame, and we give the transformation laws. On a surface, the curl of the trace term can always be removed by a frame rotation, while its divergence is proportional to the Gaussian curvature. We apply the correspondence to a Gaussian bump, where the local density of states at the center falls below its flat value and obeys a closed form at low energy; to a wire, where a nonuniform lapse binds states inside the gap and a nonuniform velocity cannot; to stationary frames on a surface, where a circulating drag shifts the Dirac mass locally with either sign; and to twisted and rotating frames in a volume, where the axial term takes the form of a chiral chemical potential produced by the frame alone, or separates Weyl nodes in a rotating sample. We separate the geometric contribution from the lattice pseudogauge field that dominates the response of strained samples

</details>


### [9] [Exact Parent Hamiltonians for Partially Magnetized Chiral Spin Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24935)
*Xin Shen,Guangyue Ji,Jie Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文基于玻色子晶格FQH态的精确Hubbard型母哈密顿量，通过取在位排斥趋于无穷并结合局域玻色子-自旋映射，构造了实现多类玻色拓扑序的部分磁化手性自旋液体的精确自旋母哈密顿量。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性自旋液体是典型的分数化相，但对应的简单精确母哈密顿量十分稀缺，亟需系统构造方法。

Method: 先建立任意排斥强度下均成立的玻色晶格分数量子霍尔态的精确Hubbard型母哈密顿量；取在位排斥U→∞并施加局域玻色子-自旋映射，得到CSL的精确母哈密顿量（双自旋构造）；进一步在带自旋Hofstadter-Hubbard模型中以二阶超交换给出可控微观实现；并推广至更一般的局域约束与相互作用以获得高自旋/多自旋母哈密顿量。

Result: 双自旋构造将Kalmeyer-Laughlin自旋哈密顿量推广到更广的玻色FQH模型态族（含阿贝尔态与非阿贝尔部分子态），并在定制跃迁与相互作用的广义Hofstadter-Hubbard模型中实现受控微观方案。

Abstract: Chiral spin liquids (CSLs) are paradigmatic fractionalized phases, yet simple exact parent Hamiltonians for them remain scarce. In this work, we construct exact spin parent Hamiltonians for partially magnetized CSLs realizing various bosonic topological orders. Our construction relies on an exact Hubbard-type parent Hamiltonian for bosonic lattice fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states, valid at arbitrary repulsive interaction strengths. Taking the on-site repulsion to infinity and applying local boson-spin mappings yields exact parent Hamiltonians for the CSLs. Our two-spin construction generalizes the Kalmeyer-Laughlin spin Hamiltonian to a broader class of bosonic FQH model states, including Abelian states and non-Abelian parton states. We derive controlled microscopic realizations of these two-spin Hamiltonians via second-order superexchange in generalized spinful Hofstadter-Hubbard models with tailored hoppings and interactions. With more general local constraints and interactions, boson-spin mappings yield exact higher-spin and multispin parent Hamiltonians for partially magnetized CSLs.

</details>

Method: 研究具有不同自旋对称性的亮孤子在坐标依赖Rabi场（类比空间变化的磁场）中的动力学；对完全Manakov对称与"提升"（lifted）Manakov对称两种情形，分析受速度扰动的孤子运动及其散射动力学；并将速度控制的散射问题与已知的Rabi淬火协议进行比较。

Result: 在坐标依赖Rabi场中，速度扰动的孤子运动在完全与提升的Manakov对称下均表现出有趣的散射动力学；速度控制散射问题与Rabi淬火协议之间存在可比性，二者比较给出强有力的孤子动力学控制途径。

Conclusion: 该工作为以孤子作为自旋载流子的非线性自旋电子学控制提供了新思路与可行方案。

Abstract: Spintronics is usually concentrated on spin-dependent dynamics of single particles such as electrons or holes in solids. Extending this concept to nonlinear matter waves offers an interesting possibility of controlling the transport of collective, particle-like excitations through their spin degrees of freedom. Recent advances in Bose-Einstein condensates have provided a versatile platform for realizing nonlinear spin-1/2 systems that support particle-like objects such as bright solitons, making studies of the motion of these solitons under external fields an interesting and important problem. Here, we study the dynamics of bright solitons with different spin symmetries moving in a coordinate-dependent Rabi field, which acts analogously to a spatially varying magnetic field. In that setup, the velocity perturbed soliton motion possesses very interesting scattering dynamics for both perfect and lifted Manakov symmetry. Lastly, we compare both the velocity-controlled scattering problem with the well-known Rabi quenching protocol. This comparison provides a powerful approach to control the soliton dynamics that may open an avenue to control nonlinear spintronics with solitons as spin carriers.

</details>

Result: 曲率梯度耦合强度由剪切模量与曲率梯度决定；剪切可驱动超流；在椭球面上得到几何选择定则与多重态劈裂；模式交叉附近弱形变产生超流与晶格动能相当的杂化模式，剪切表现为密度响应。

Abstract: Supersolids on curved surfaces are becoming experimentally accessible. We develop a Galilean-covariant effective field theory of supersolids on curved surfaces and show that gradients of Gaussian curvature couple lattice shear to compression with a strength fixed by the shear modulus and the curvature gradient. As a result, shear drives superflow. On spheroids this yields geometric selection rules and multiplet splittings; near mode crossings, weak deformations produce hybrids in which superfluid and lattice carry comparable kinetic energy, and shear becomes visible as a density response.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出以光晶格中超冷玻色子（Bose-Hubbard模型）为工质的量子热机，在类Otto循环中引入改变相互作用强度的冲程；利用将强排斥工质绝热压缩至Mott绝缘体相可同时获得最大功输出与近卡诺效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子热机研究多聚焦于非相互作用或可分离工质，相互作用多被视为耗散或复杂性来源。作者希望探究强关联量子多体相互作用（乃至跨越量子相变）能否作为可控的热力学资源来提升热机性能。

Method: 将N个相互作用超冷玻色子约束于一维光晶格，用Bose-Hubbard哈密顿量描述；构造类Otto循环，除常规的等容热化与压缩/膨胀冲程外，增加改变玻色子间相互作用强度（U）的冲程，并在Mott绝缘体与超流等量子相之间切换；通过计算功输出与效率并与非相互作用循环对比来分析性能。

Result: 相互作用循环优于非相互作用循环；作为热机时，在强排斥区将工质压缩至Mott绝缘体态可获得最大功输出与接近卡诺极限的效率；压缩冲程后减弱相互作用有利于并增强热机运行，而增强相互作用则有利于制冷循环。

Conclusion: 跨越物质量子相控制相互作用强度可作为一种有效的热力学资源，用于优化量子热机的功输出与效率，并可通过调节相互作用方向（增强/减弱）在热机与制冷模式间进行选择性偏好设计。

Abstract: This work proposes a quantum thermal machine comprised of interacting ultracold bosons confined in a one-dimensional optical lattice and modeled by the Bose-Hubbard Hamiltonian. We consider an Otto-inspired cycle with strokes that change the interaction strength between bosons, in addition to standard thermalization and compression/expansion strokes. We find that these interaction changes can be manipulated to enhance the performance of the thermal machine, outperforming non-interacting cycles. In particular, when operating as a heat engine, the cycle can achieve its largest work output and near-Carnot efficiency by compressing the medium at strong repulsion into a Mott insulator state. Additionally, we find that decreasing interactions after the compression stroke favors and enhances the engine operation, whereas increasing interactions favors refrigeration cycles. The findings of this work further show that controlling the interactions across quantum phases of matter can be harnessed as a useful thermodynamic resource.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用基于linked-cluster展开的场论方法，仔细识别共振声子-杂质散射过程，对零温信号的热致指数衰减给出大时间渐近估计，区分准粒子区与红外主导区两类动力学情形。

Result: 在准粒子区（由宿主杂质有限寿命主导）和红外主导区（杂质引发Anderson正交灾难）均得到热致指数衰减的大时间渐近形式，并各自识别出温度诱导的相干时间，其倒数决定零温单粒子格林函数振幅的指数修正时标。

Abstract: We consider the problem of a quadratically-dispersing quantum impurity interacting through a contact density-density term with a one-dimensional (1D) superfluid described by Luttinger liquid theory. By means of a field-theoretical approach based on the linked-cluster expansion, we quantitatively address the effects of a finite temperature of the 1D bath on the momentum-space single-particle Green's function. Through a careful identification of the resonant phonon-impurity scattering processes, we obtain asymptotic large-time estimates for the thermally-induced exponential damping of the zero-temperature signal both in the $\textit{quasiparticle}$ regime, dominated by the presence of a finite lifetime for the host impurity, and in the $\textit{infrared-dominated}$ regime, where the impurity causes a Anderson's orthogonality catastrophe of the 1D bath. In both cases, we identify a temperature-induced coherence time that sets the characteristic timescale of the exponentially-decaying finite-temperature correction to the amplitude of the zero-temperature single-particle Green's function. Moreover, we draw connections between the aforesaid thermal timescale and the microscopic parameters of the Lieb-Liniger model, which admits an effective Luttinger-liquid description, highlighting the dependence of the inverse coherence time on the intra-bath interactions across the different dynamical regimes of the impurity.

</details>

Abstract: Coherent control of fermions has become a standard approach for engineering quantum devices. Here, we explore parity-induced frustration in multiterminal Josephson arrays. We focus on odd-parity Josephson trijunctions, where the presence of a trapped fermion induces a phase winding in each trijunction. Connecting these trijunctions constrains their shared phases, resulting in a long-distance phase coherence where the frustrated configurations dominate the low-energy physics. In the classical regime, these arrays map onto the Kagome XY antiferromagnet, whose established Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) transition yields algebraically decaying $6e$ phase correlations. We show that the $6e$ phase coherence is manifested as a pronounced third harmonic in the current-phase relation of these arrays. While quantum phase fluctuations suppress the $6e$ coherence, we show that through spin-orbit coupling they also lead to spin correlations and entanglement between the trapped fermions. Our results establish fermion-induced frustration as a route to composite superconducting coherence and phase-mediated spin entanglement in superconductor-semiconductor artificial quantum materials.

</details>

Abstract: We put forward a real-space linear-response theory of orbital magnetization induced by a dc electric field in bulk materials lacking translation invariance. Starting from the current response to a slowly varying electromagnetic field, we show that the linear-in-wavevector conductivity tensor of non-interacting electrons in terms of single-particle Green's functions and the velocity and position operators. This provides a gauge-invariant real-space formulation of the long-wavelength non-equilibrium orbital magnetization of both perfect crystals and disordered systems. In the translationally invariant limit, the formalism recovers the established Bloch picture of orbital magneto-electric effects. We implement the formalism using large-scale Chebyshev expansions and illustrate its capabilities on a Haldane model in the presence of spatially uncorrelated disorder.

</details>

Abstract: Orbital magnetization is a fundamental measure of time-reversal symmetry breaking in solids that is accessible by experiments. However, unlike the well-studied equilibrium case, the understanding of orbital magnetism resulting from external stimuli such as light and electric fields is still in its infancy. Here we employ a novel real-space framework to investigate charge-to-orbital conversion in a metallic orbital antiferromagnet beyond the conventional Bloch-band picture. We find that common point defects give rise to strongly enhanced time-odd magnetoelectric effects and, depending on their local symmetry, further activate otherwise-forbidden time-even responses. Moreover, we introduce a local marker that provides spatially resolved information on the orbital magnetization carried by non-equilibrium states. Real-space maps reveal that point defects create nanoscale hotspots of orbital magnetization leading to a profound modification of the bulk magnetoelectric response, including a sign reversal of the net orbital moment. Our findings may open up the possibility of engineering linear-response orbital moments with atomic-scale precision by point defects and impurities.

TL;DR: 本文将可机械剥离的非平面自旋交叉分子FTBQS与单层TMD、hBN组装成异质结，通过光谱证实存在长程Förster共振能量转移及由能带对齐驱动的电荷转移。

Abstract: We investigate the spontaneous emission of a quantum emitter placed near finite atomic chains described by the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model. The resonant enhancement depends on both spectral matching and the emitter position relative to the induced charge and field patterns. In metallic chains, the nearly harmonic energy spectrum leads to overlapping optical transitions and strong resonances. Coulomb interactions affect the optical resonances through blueshift or modification of their spatial character, and act differently on collective and single-particle-like excitations. Finally, we show that the same mechanisms demonstrated in atomic chains persist in realistic quasi-one-dimensional systems, including trans-polyacetylene chains and graphene nanoribbons. The softer vibrational spectrum of the high-temperature phase yields the entropy gain that stabilizes this phase above the critical temperature $T_\mathrm{c}$. Evaluating the thermodynamic balance between internal energy and entropy at $T_\mathrm{c} = 125\,\mathrm{K}$ yields a zero-temperature energy difference of $ΔE_0 = 138\,\mathrm{meV}/(8{\times}2)$, including the difference in vibrational zero-point energies of the two phases, thus providing direct access to hidden thermodynamic parameters of a first-order surface phase transition.

Abstract: Ground-state energy estimation (GSEE) and ground-state preparation (GSP) are fundamental problems in quantum computing. Given a Hamiltonian $H$ with $\|H\|\le1$ and a guiding state $|ψ\rangle$ with overlap at least $γ>0$ with the ground space of $H$, the goal in GSEE is to estimate the ground-state energy of $H$ to additive error $δ$ with failure probability at most $\varepsilon$, while in GSP it is to prepare a state with ground-space weight at least $1-\varepsilon$, given a gap lower bound $Δ$. Their costs are measured by quantum query complexity, i.e., the number of oracle calls needed to access $H$. Recent work proved optimal bounds $Θ(\log(1/\varepsilon)/(γδ))$ for GSEE and $Θ(\log(1/\varepsilon)/(γΔ))$ for GSP in the time-evolution and block-encoding models. However, these models do not directly account for the sparsity of $H$, measured by the maximum number $s$ of nonzero entries in any row or column. In the sparse-access model, oracles instead return the locations and values of these entries. This introduces the additional parameter $s$, and it remained open how query complexity depends jointly on $s$, $γ$, $δ$ or $Δ$, and $\varepsilon$. We resolve this by proving sparse-query lower bounds $Ω(\sqrt{s}\log(1/\varepsilon)/(γδ))$ for GSEE and $Ω(\sqrt{s}\log(1/\varepsilon)/(γΔ))$ for GSP, even for stoquastic Hamiltonians. We also show that these constructions recover the earlier time-evolution and block-encoding lower-bound scalings. We then give matching upper-bound algorithms in the reversible in-place sparse-location model. Thus, we obtain $Θ(\sqrt{s}\log(1/\varepsilon)/(γδ))$ for GSEE and $Θ(\sqrt{s}\log(1/\varepsilon)/(γΔ))$ for GSP, showing that $\sqrt{s}$ is necessary and sufficient and completing the joint dependence on all problem parameters.

</details>

The quantity $p_γ$ is a coupling-dependent counterfactual mismatch probability: it bounds a contextual Bell expression, but neither generates nor predicts the quantum Bell correlation. We therefore test path memory through the observable difference between direct and looped implementations, using a no-signaling phenomenological response in which a hidden holonomy $δ_γ$ modifies one correlation term in $S_γ^{\rm mix}$. Our results thus shed light on the highly subtle interplay between quantum resources and the computational difficulty encountered by QMC methods.

Abstract: The fractured low-energy landscape of spin glasses presents a notorious barrier to optimization. Here, we treat the QAOA distribution as a statistical-physics object and probe its geometry directly in the thermodynamic limit. We find that typical QAOA samples remain macroscopically uncorrelated, even as their sensitivity to this coupling grows with circuit depth $p$. By contrast, the low-energy tail shows a much stronger tendency to overlap and reveals a Parisi-like phase with the same critical behavior as the thermal spin-glass transition. The onset of this organization moves toward the typical output as the circuit deepens, consistent with the separation vanishing only as $p\to\infty$. Abstract: Quantum annealing is a promising quantum algorithm for dealing with computationally hard optimization problems. Currently available hardware can implement the algorithm for a significant class of Hamiltonian problems. Abstract: Monge-Kantorovich optimal transport is a mathematical framework that determines the most efficient way to transform one probability distribution into another while minimizing a given cost function. Its central constructions include the Wasserstein distances, which quantify the space of probability distributions, and dual formulations that link to convex analysis. Its standard quantum generalization replaces probability distributions with density operators and cost function with positive semidefinite cost operator, which should be regarded as spatial quantum optimal transport. In this work, we propose the quantum optimal transport problem in spacetime through the spatiotemporal phase-space Kirkwood-Dirac quasiprobability distribution and spatiotemporal states and through spatiotemporal state. We show that the two formalisms are equivalent and related via spatiotemporal operator frames. In the Kirkwood-Dirac phase-space formalism, the spatiotemporal distribution and the cost function are complex-valued, and the spatiotemporal couplings are Kirkwood-Dirac quasiprobability distributions that yield the correct marginals for the local states. In the spatiotemporal state formalism, the cost operators are generally non-Hermitian, and the spatiotemporal couplings are generally non-Hermitian and yield the correct reduced local states. The Margenau-Hill phase-space distribution gives rise to a real-valued optimal transport in spatiotemporal phase space; in the spatiotemporal state formalism, this corresponds to a Hermitian spatiotemporal coupling state. We give a rigorous definitions and a careful discussion of its duality. We also introduce a spatiotemporal quantum Wasserstein metric based on a metric in spatiotemporal phase spaces and show that it satisfies the metric axioms.

Abstract: Quantum charge-coupled device (QCCD) architectures enable scalable trapped-ion quantum computing by dynamically shuttling ions between specialized processing and storage zones. A QCCD can be built with many trap sizes and layouts. Comparing them requires compiling every program under each design's transport, capacity and control constraints, and trusting the target code of each compilation. We present a co-design framework built on one Lean-verified compiler. The hardware description and its operating rules are compiler inputs, so a single theorem covers every candidate design: the target code of every compilation preserves its program's behavior and obeys the hardware rules. Across 75 benchmark programs of 11 to 130 qubits, the best design found takes 66% less modeled execution time on average than the worst design of equal capacity. An AI agent that proposes devices based on our compiler's checked results beats the fastest of these designs on 71 of 75 programs. Our verified compiler provably avoids the 11 defects that we report in three previous compilers. Abstract: Quantum singular value transformation (QSVT) implements matrix inversion using bounded polynomials. Increasing polynomial degree can improve normalization and reduce amplified query cost, with savings that depend on the error criterion. A simpler two-term rule also has certified savings, and its phase factors at condition number ten thousand pass numerical checks at selected points. For residual error, a sparse Chebyshev construction achieves certified savings over the residual-minimax baseline of at least 8.7 percent at condition number ten thousand with normalized-state error at most one hundred-millionth, and at least 13.1 percent at one million with error at most one ten-billionth.   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 以自旋单态一半作移动ancilla并配合末端Bell测量flag，是抑制shuttled自旋量子比特中ancilla故障扩散、恢复并提升高权重qLDPC码电路距离的有效方案，显著降低逻辑错误率且对读出误差与比特翻转具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: High-rate quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes could greatly reduce the qubit count of silicon spin-qubit processors, but their checks are long-range and of high weight. Shuttling one ancilla electron through every data qubit of a check measures such checks, but a single fault on the ancilla spreads to several data qubits, so the circuit distance can fall far below the code distance. We make the mobile ancilla one half of a spin singlet whose partner stays at home; a Bell measurement at the end of the check provides a flag for isolated bit-flip faults acquired during the walk. Using new distance-6 codes with check weights 8, 10 and 12, and exact circuit distances from two independent solvers, we show that without the flag the circuit distance falls as check weight grows, to 1 at weight 12, while with it every weight-8 and weight-10 code recovers circuit distance 5 or 6 and corrects as many faults as the code. For codes of distance 8 to 12 the flag raises the circuit distance by one to four, and the Gross code [[144,12,12]] rises from 7 to 10. In circuit-level simulations of shuttled spin qubits, the flag lowers logical error rates by typically 2-10x across bivariate-bicycle, circulant-permutation and surface codes, and the gain survives percent-level flag-readout errors and bit flips on the singlet's electrons. BP+OSD does not reach the full circuit distance of the flagged circuits, so our flagged logical error rates are upper bounds: decoding the same shots exactly raises the flag's gain on the distance-6 codes from 3-7x to 5-17x for the weight-6 BB code, and from 1-12x to 5-90x at weights 8 and 10 for p >= 2.5x10^-4.

Conclusion: VAP 是一种资源高效、可扩展的量子态纯化方法，适用于近期量子设备。

Abstract: We introduce virtual autopurification (VAP), a protocol for virtual purification that operates entirely within a single translationally invariant system. Virtual purification suppresses thermal fluctuations and decoherence noise by amplifying the dominant pure component of a mixed state, but its standard implementation requires multiple copies of the state and global entangled measurements. VAP avoids the multiple-copy overhead by constructing virtual copies from spatially separated subregions and performing entangled measurements only between them. Assuming clustering of correlations, we show that VAP reproduces the expectation values of the standard virtual purification protocol, with discrepancies that decay exponentially in both the buffer length and the inter-subregion distance. Numerical simulations support the theoretical predictions and demonstrate accurate implementation of virtual cooling with reduced resource requirements. Our results establish VAP as a resource-efficient and scalable approach to quantum-state purification in near-term devices.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

With all logical qubits outside these triples initialized to $|0\rangle$, any subset of the corresponding logical CCZ gates can be implemented with a transversal physical CCZ circuit. As applications, we obtain two nearly optimal magic state protocols: CCZ distillation with arbitrarily small overhead exponent $γ\rightarrow 0$ and error suppression of nearly linear order, and a magic state fountain that directly prepares $N^{1-ε}$ encoded CCZ magic states with nearly linear distance $Ω_ε(N/\log^2N)$. The construction uses balanced products of expanding graph covers. We design the expanding graph covers to admit many common classical codewords, which yield logical operators whose pairings select the logical CCZ gates independently. Without the product expansion assumption, the graph covers also yield classical LDPC codes over a fixed field with almost linear dimension and linear distance that satisfy a multiplication property: the coordinatewise product of any three codewords lies in a second LDPC code of linear distance.

Abstract: Quantum Computing uses quantum mechanical phenomena, such as superposition and entanglement of quantum bits (qubits), to represent and manipulate information in multidimensional state spaces. This approach allows complex mathematical functions to be executed simultaneously rather than sequentially, making them much more efficient than classical computers. However, current quantum computers suffer from quantum decoherence, where small changes to their environment, such as thermal fluctuations and vibrations, affect the systems' fragile states. The goal of this study is to discuss the functionality of a proposed Fe$^{2+}$:forsterite qubit system by solving the unitary time-evolution of a quantum state vector for the system's Hamiltonian, using known axial and traverse anisotropy terms at temperatures up to 10 K. The system can effectively transfer population between its two lowest states at rates predicted by the Rabi half-period, with 99.9% fidelity. Additionally, it can hold the population at a desired state without docohering. The results remained consistent for temperatures up to 10 K, meaning, unlike many common qubit systems, the proposed qubit system could function without a dilution fridge and is not fragile to thermal noise.

Abstract: One of the greatest merits of cavity magnonics (CM) is the ability to explore rich coupled-mode physics, driven by the large \textit{in situ} flux tunability of magnonic resonances and magnon--photon coupling. Meanwhile, van der Waals (vdW) magnets offer an ultra-compact and highly integrated implementation of CM without complex fabrication processes. Nonetheless, the substantial magnon losses significantly hinder the combination of all these advantages. We achieve an order-of-magnitude reduction in loss while maintaining a strong coupling condition. Additionally, we theoretically confirm the feasibility of simultaneous and \textit{in situ} tuning of both loss and coupling by varying the magnetic field direction and strength. Featuring a micrometer-scale thickness and a width of a few millimeters, our device is significantly smaller than those based on bulk crystals or crystal arrays. Our results establish vdW cavity magnonics as versatile platform for investigating intriguing phenomena predicted in coupled-mode systems.

Abstract: Spectral bounds for Markov generators require a choice of time scale. In quantum dynamics, a rate read off from a jump representation, the escape rate from a pure state and the smallest spectral disk need not agree. We prove that the spectrum of every GKLS generator written without a Hamiltonian lies in a disk tangent to the imaginary axis, with radius given by the norm of the operator $Φ^\dagger(I)$ built from the jump map $Φ$. This settles the first two scaling conjectures of "Corralling spectra of generators of time-continuous Markov evolution (both classical and quantum)", J. Phys. A: Math. Theor. {59}, 175202 (2026). The third, escape-rate conjecture fails for a qutrit with a single normal jump. Nevertheless, twice the escape rate bounds every decay rate of any positive trace-preserving semigroup For GKLS generators with vanishing canonical Hamiltonian, four times the escape rate always supplies a spectral disk, and the factor four is optimal uniformly in dimension. We compare these bounds with classical Cassini ovals and thermodynamic ellipses, explain the obstacles to their quantum extensions, and formulate optimization over completely positive jump-map representations as a semidefinite program.

In a benchmark against the exact microscopic dynamics of two coupled oscillators, our equation maintains $O(λ^2)$ accuracy across secular and nonsecular regimes, outperforming the existing detailed-balance equations tested. The only techniques known for such groups scale exponentially in the size of the input. We first study the group $U_4(\mathbb{F}_p)$, the smallest upper unitriangular group exhibiting nilpotency class three. We analyze the application of the Pretty Good Measurement approach used for Heisenberg groups and show its limits. Abstract: Resonantly driven two-level quantum batteries (QBs) suffer from strong pulse-area dependence and energy backflow inherent to Rabi oscillations. To address this limitation, we propose charging of a two-level QB with chirped pulses. We investigate four charging protocols formed by pairing hyperbolic secant (sech) or Gaussian pulse envelopes with linear or hyperbolic-tangent (tanh) frequency sweeps. We show that sufficiently strong chirp modulation converts the charging mechanism from pulse-area-governed Rabi rotation to adiabatic rapid passage. We analyze the underlying adiabatic dynamics and derive the criteria for robust chirped charging, which incorporate the sweep bandwidth, nonadiabatic transitions, and dissipative effects. Under strong chirp modulation, the final stored energy and ergotropy converge to stable plateaus that are nearly insensitive to the pulse area across an extended parameter window. Of the four schemes, the sech pulse with tanh chirp delivers the widest ergotropy plateau and the weakest pulse area sensitivity. Compared with counterdiabatic driving, the sech tanh protocol attains stable charging performance without demanding an extra drive quadrature. Furthermore, this chirp-enabled charging is resilient to static detuning and weak-to-moderate dissipation. This work establishes a practical route toward robust charging of quantum batteries using chirped pulse control.

Abstract: Post-selection lowers the logical error rate of a quantum error-correcting code by rejecting unreliable shots. For a code with $K$ logical observables, the logical gap, a standard post-selection criterion, involves $2^K$ logical classes, and existing approximations to it suffer from a non-convergence error floor or a shielding effect. We introduce a decoder-agnostic post-selection strategy for quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes, called parity push post-selection (PPPS), which re-decodes a shot once per logical observable with a push toward the opposite parity. Each push adds one check node and one variable node to the Tanner graph of the detector error model, and we accept a shot only when every re-decoding returns a solution in the original logical class. We show that, with degenerate maximum-likelihood decoding, PPPS accepts a shot exactly when the logical gap exceeds the push strength $λ$. Applied with the Relay-BP decoder to the [[72,12,6]] bivariate bicycle code at $p=10^{-3}$ and the [[144,12,12]] code at $p=3\times10^{-3}$ under circuit-level depolarizing noise, PPPS lowers the logical error rate by factors of about 50 and 75, respectively, at rejection rates near 1%. We observe that PPPS rejects failures that prior post-selection strategies cannot reject, and thus shows improved performance.

</details>

Abstract: We present a relational framework for characterizing the evolution of quantum composite systems, based on the correlations within a history state describing the whole evolution relative to a quantum reference clock. While total system-clock entanglement entropy consistently measures the unitary evolution of the closed global system, a different picture emerges for the reduced state of an open subsystem, whose evolution is generally non-unitary due to environmental coupling. We introduce a general formalism that quantifies open-subsystem dynamics using a subsystem-clock mutual information derived from the history state. We demonstrate that this quantity serves as a consistent quantifier of subsystem evolution with respect to a given clock basis, can be applied to arbitrary dynamics, and reduces to system-time entanglement entropy in the global limit. Furthermore, maximizing this measure over all clock bases yields a basis-independent bound on the maximum evolution achievable relative to the clock, directly linked to the difference between the subsystem's entanglement with the rest of the history state and its entanglement with the environment. Finally, we derive analytical expressions for qubit clocks, illustrate our results using spin 1/2 and spin 1 quantum walks, and present a full matrix formalism of Kraus operators for describing the most general subsystem evolution.

Result: 在 GHZ 与 Harris-Ross 实例上验证了端到端重构，并在结构化 GHZ 模拟器工作负载上评估了系统与宽度间的权衡。

Conclusion: QWeaver 将线路切割的切割、并发执行与准概率重构统一为任务图驱动的编程与编排工作流，实现端到端可用的量子线路分布式执行。

Abstract: Quantum computers available today are constrained by limited qubit counts and high noise levels, making the direct execution of large quantum circuits infeasible on a single device, and while quantum circuit cutting addresses this by partitioning a large circuit into smaller subcircuits for independent execution, orchestrating the full end-to-end workflow remains a significant programming challenge. In this paper, we present QWeaver, a task-graph-based programming and orchestration workflow framework for quantum circuit cutting that accepts OpenQASM quantum circuits as input and constructs a directed acyclic task graph. In this graph, the Multi-Level Intermediate Representation (MLIR)-based circuit cut is the first task, responsible for analyzing and partitioning the input circuit into executable fragment representations; the generated fragments are then handed to a set of quantum circuit execution tasks that depend on the cut task and can be dispatched concurrently across available quantum backends via the Quantum Intelligent Runtime System (Q-IRIS). Finally, a weaving task, dependent on all execution tasks, performs classical post-processing that applies quasi-probability reconstruction techniques to combine the sampled measurement outcomes of all fragments and recover an accurate approximation of the original circuit's output distribution. We describe the architecture of QWeaver, its integration with Q-IRIS and the execution engine for the Quantum Intermediate Representation (QIR-EE), validate end-to-end reconstruction on GHZ and Harris-Ross instances, and evaluate systems and width trade-offs on structured GHZ simulator workloads.

</details>

Abstract: The performance of a quantum battery depends not only on how much energy it stores, but also on how rapidly useful charge can be restored after work extraction. We show how engineering the reservoir spectrum connects these properties in an interacting collective-spin battery. Interactions separate the Dicke transition frequencies, allowing frequency-selective pumping and loss to control where excitation accumulates and how populations move along the ladder. Analytical results relate the pump-to-loss ratio to charge localization, fluctuations, and extractable work. Two reservoirs producing the same mean charge can nevertheless exhibit markedly different localization and charging times. Under repeated extraction, finite-time recharging also generates correlations between successive work outputs. At matched mean work, these correlations can reverse the ordering of single-cycle and long-time fluctuations: a protocol with larger fluctuations in individual strokes can deliver more reliable accumulated work. An exact counting relation connects these statistics to net charging-energy transfer, while relaxation modes distinguish recovery of the stationary state from recovery of useful work. Finally, local dephasing redistributes population among collective-spin sectors, leading to an exact stationary symmetry-dilution law and a finite operating window for work extraction under a collective transverse controller.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典时间序列分析通过平稳性、遍历性、谱表示与有限记忆模型，把时间依赖性转化为可由单条观测轨迹学习的结构；量子实验本质上是时间序列（每次运行都是干预与结果的序列，开放系统的记忆写在其时间统计中）。但经典基础无法直接移植：对过程进行测量并遗忘结果，与完全不测量并不相同，因此不存在单一族联合概率分布可描述量子过程。

Method: 采用过程张量（process tensor）框架刻画平稳量子时间序列；针对相对固定且信息完备的因果断开（causal break）具有有限量子马尔可夫阶 l 的平稳梳（stationary combs），构造有限维因果转移映射（causal transfer map）。

Result: 对有限量子马尔可夫阶 l 的平稳梳，所构造的因果转移映射维度至多为 d^{4l}，其中 d 为每一步系统希尔伯特空间维数的上界。

Conclusion: 上述结果为量子谱分析、模型辨识与单轨迹推断奠定了平稳性基础。

Abstract: Classical time series analysis converts temporal dependence into structure that can be learned from a single observed trajectory, through stationarity, ergodicity, spectral representation, and finite-memory models. Quantum experiments are naturally time series: every run is a sequence of interventions and outcomes, and the memory of an open system is written in its temporal statistics. However, the classical foundation cannot be translated directly: measuring a process and forgetting the outcome differs from not measuring at all, so no single family of joint probability distributions describes a quantum process. We develop a process-tensor framework for stationary quantum time series and establish its first structural theorems. For stationary combs of finite quantum Markov order l relative to a fixed informationally complete causal break, we construct a finite-dimensional causal transfer map of dimension at most d^{4l}, where d bounds the system Hilbert-space dimension at each step. ... These results supply the stationary foundation on which quantum spectral analysis, model identification, and single-trajectory inference can be built.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层析与shadow估计依赖Pauli观测量，但实验是一次选一个测量设置（每比特赋X/Y/Z基），一次运行的设置同时"覆盖"多个相容的Pauli观测量，因此需弄清随机设置能覆盖多大比例的观测量，以及被同一设置覆盖的观测量共享结果而产生相关性对估计的影响。

Method: 将覆盖问题刻画为覆盖率随设置数指数增长的锐利阈值，增长率由零失真下的率失真函数给出（每比特3^{3/4}）；推导达到目标覆盖比例所需设置数的显式表达式（余项在小振荡内）；引入设置的设计效应（等于该设置捕获的Pauli期望值的平方）量化设置内估计相关性，推导其对所有态的闭式均值，并给出可由测量数据无偏估计的量；进行6比特数值模拟，比较设置测量与独立测量、平均估计与直方图拟合。

Result: 覆盖率在每比特3^{3/4}增长率处出现锐利阈值；随机采样渐近最优，差距因子小于1.69；设计效应可闭式求均值并无偏估计；6比特模拟中设置测量比独立测量同样数量观测量少7.6–38倍线路运行；高覆盖时直方图拟合比平均估计约减半重建误差，主因是拟合计入每个观测量被多少设置覆盖；相关性使该拟合约付出2倍代价，设计效应越大越值得建模。

Conclusion: 随机测量设置是覆盖Pauli观测量近最优的方案，覆盖阈值由率失真函数决定；估计误差不仅取决于覆盖，还取决于设置内相关性，而设计效应可作为判断是否建模相关性、以及选择拟合方式的可操作指标。

Abstract: Quantum state tomography and shadow-based estimation rely on measuring Pauli observables. An experiment does not measure them one at a time: it chooses a measurement setting that assigns one of the bases X, Y or Z to each qubit, and a single run in that setting yields estimates of every Pauli observable compatible with it. We say that the setting covers those observables. We determine how many random settings cover a prescribed fraction of all Pauli observables. As the number of settings grows exponentially with the number of qubits, the covered fraction has a sharp threshold at a growth rate of $3^{3/4}$ per qubit, set by the rate-distortion function of covering at zero distortion. We give an explicit expression for the number of settings needed to reach a target fraction, accurate up to a remainder that reduces to a small oscillation for many qubits, and show that random sampling is asymptotically optimal up to a factor of less than 1.69. Coverage does not determine what a setting contributes to an estimator. Observables covered by one setting are estimated from the same outcomes, so their estimates are correlated. We quantify this correlation through the design effect of a setting, which equals the squared Pauli expectation that the setting captures and depends on the target state. We derive its mean over settings in closed form for every state and show that it can be estimated without bias from the measurement data. In simulations on six qubits, measuring settings needs 7.6 to 38 times fewer circuit runs than measuring the same number of observables independently. At high coverage, fitting the outcome histograms instead of averaged estimates roughly halves the reconstruction error, mainly because the fit counts how many settings cover each observable. The correlation itself costs such a fit about a factor of two, and a larger design effect signals more to gain from modeling it.

</details>

Near half a flux quantum, the two paths to complete reversal interfere destructively: in microscopic simulations, a slow uniform sweep reverses one field with probability 0.998, although the final bias favors reversing both. A four-state model calibrated at zero bias predicts how the window of this Aharonov-Bohm blockade narrows with sweep time. The blocked state remains coherent. Abstract: Spatially varying magnetic fields steer charged particles along directed paths. Where a field reverses sign, particles follow snake-like orbits along the reversal line, in a direction set by both the field orientation and the sign of their energy. Transport measurements have detected such snake states, but imaging their dynamics in a reprogrammable field landscape remains challenging. Here we digitally simulate the Harper--Hofstadter model with programmable synthetic magnetic fields on a 49-qubit superconducting processor. In each Trotter step, every hopping term is one native fractional-iSWAP gate, and single-qubit $Z$ rotations set all 36 plaquette fluxes independently, without additional entangling gates. Uniform, random, and stepped flux landscapes reveal Aharonov--Bohm suppression of diagonal propagation, disorder-induced slowing, and magnetic reflection. At a field reversal, bonding and antibonding initial states, which carry opposite energy biases, propagate in opposite directions, and both directions reverse when the field is inverted. A symmetry analysis of these reversals yields a chiral displacement of nearly one lattice spacing after ten Trotter rounds, with symmetry-breaking residuals below 0.05 lattice spacings. </details>

TL;DR: 提出一种受经典处理器启发的容错量子计算架构：量子芯片仅通过常数条外部引线接收经典指令流，其余量子比特在周期性的、几何局域的固定深度-3电路下演化，并给出精确阈值定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固态量子处理器每个量子比特都需专用控制线从片外接收指令，纠错还产生大量需离片的综合征数据，导致外部控制规模随比特数线性膨胀，成为扩展瓶颈。经典处理器用极少外部引线控制数百亿晶体管，这一反差启发了本文。

Method: 设计"量子数据流"式容错架构：芯片通过常数条外部引线（每条耦合单个量子比特）接收经典指令输入流；除这些接口比特外，全部量子比特在制造时即固定的、周期性施加的几何局域深度-3电路下演化，由本地门驱动量子信息在平面阵列中流动；假设存在无噪声全局时钟以保证所有操作按时执行。在此模型下证明容错精度阈值定理。

Result: 在每门、每次测量与每条线路均受低于普适阈值的物理噪声影响时，n 个物理比特的芯片可运行使用 n/polylog(n/ε) 个逻辑比特、每逻辑门错误率 ε 的任意量子算法；每逻辑门耗时 n/polylog(n/ε)。若采用量子操作保持严格局域且有噪声、但片上线路可瞬时广播无噪声经典信号的替代设计，每逻辑门耗时降至 polylog(n/ε)。

Conclusion: 量子芯片的外部控制可由随比特数线性增长简化为常数规模，代价是逻辑开销与时间开销（多项式或对数多项式因子），从而在保持容错性的前提下实现类似经典处理器的"少引线、内部数据流"架构。

Abstract: Contemporary classical information processors integrate tens of billions of transistors printed on a chip, yet are fully controlled through comparatively few external wires. This is possible because data flows through hard-coded operations in step with a global clock and is steered by external instructions. Today's solid-state quantum processors are different -- every qubit receives instructions from outside the chip via its own dedicated control line, and error correction adds a further torrent of syndrome data that is transported off-chip. Here we show that the external control of a quantum chip can be greatly simplified. We propose a fault-tolerant architecture for universal quantum computation, inspired by modern classical processors, in which quantum information flows through a fixed, clocked device, propelled by local gates in a planar array. Our quantum processor receives an input stream of classical instructions via a small, constant number of external wires, each attached to a single qubit. Except for those few qubits coupled to external wires, all qubits evolve under a periodically-applied geometrically-local depth-3 circuit, which is fixed at the time the device is fabricated. Assuming a noiseless global clock (so that every operation is applied on schedule, though imperfectly), we establish an accuracy threshold theorem: For physical noise below a universal threshold -- afflicting every gate, measurement, and wire -- a chip with $n$ physical qubits can run any quantum algorithm that uses~$n/{\sf polylog}(n/ε)$ logical qubits, where $ε$ is the error rate per logical gate. Each logical gate takes time~$n/{\sf polylog}(n/ε)$; an alternative design, in which quantum operations remain strictly local and noisy, but on-chip wires broadcast noiseless classical signals instantaneously, reduces the time per logical gate to ${\sf polylog}(n/ε)$.

</details>

*Louis Follet,Paavai Pari,Isidor Schoch,Thien Nguyen,Justin G. Lietz,Daniel Lowell,Brucek Khailany,Krysta M. Svore,Dirk R. Englund,Taylor L. Patti*

TL;DR: 提出PTSBE的无近似推广，支持含mid-circuit测量与一般Kraus信道的噪声电路精确轨迹采样，在GPU/CUDA-Q上实现，获得最高83倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子协议研究（如训练量子纠错机器学习解码器）需要从含噪量子电路高速采样大量测量数据。精确模拟真实噪声代价高昂，而基于稳定子/Pauli twirling的模拟虽快却改变了输出统计分布，引入近似误差。原有PTSBE方法要求每个随机事件的结果概率与量子态无关，因而无法处理mid-circuit测量和一般Kraus信道。

Method: 将PTSBE推广至测量与Kraus算符且不引入近似：对事件进行部分预采样，对延后确定的结果在分组执行阶段通过多项式分支（multinomial branching）与缓存态复用抽取；在GPU上以CUDA-Q扩展的形式实现。

Conclusion: 该方法使通用含噪电路的高吞吐精确模拟变得实用，可支撑解码器训练数据生成与真实噪声下基于测量的容错协议研究，并揭示Pauli twirling会系统性扭曲噪声统计。

Abstract: Many studies of quantum protocols require fast sampling of large amounts of measurement data from noisy quantum circuits, such as for training machine-learning decoders for quantum error correction. Exact simulation of realistic noise is expensive, while stabilizer-based simulators are fast but approximate, for example through Pauli twirling, which generally alters the output statistics. Pre-trajectory sampling with batched execution (PTSBE) accelerates exact trajectory simulation by sampling noise before state evolution. This method requires the outcome probability of every stochastic event to be independent of the state, which excludes mid-circuit measurements and general Kraus channels. We present a generalization of PTSBE to measurement and Kraus operators without approximation. Each event is partially pre-sampled, while deferred outcomes are drawn during grouped execution via multinomial branching and cached-state reuse. We implement the method for GPUs as an extension of CUDA-Q. On distance-3 topological code memories under amplitude damping, it is up to 24x faster than conventional trajectory sampling in CUDA-Q. On 30-qubit random circuits with mid-circuit measurements, it is up to 83x faster and outperforms the near-Clifford simulator Clifft when more than about 25% of the gates are non-Clifford. With repeated sampling of each final state, its sampling rate per trajectory exceeds that of CUDA-Q by nearly five orders of magnitude. Our method also allows us to compare exact amplitude damping with its Pauli-twirled channel on a surface-code memory, revealing that twirling overestimates the detection rate by 21% and raises the logical error rate of a lookup decoder by 8%. This work makes exact, high-throughput simulation practical for general noisy circuits, from data generation for training decoders to studying measurement-based fault-tolerant protocols under realistic noise.

Conclusion: 顺序+绝热范式的融合可在特定NP完全问题上实现超平方根加速，加速幅度由子句连通性等结构参数决定。

Abstract: By intertwining ideas from sequential and adiabatic quantum computing, we propose a quantum algorithm for the problem class Exact Cover 3, which is a sub-class of 3-satisfiability (3-SAT) problems and NP (nondeterministic polynomial time) complete. We find a quantum run-time which is significantly faster than the square-root run-time $N\to N^{1/2}$ of the Grover algorithm. </details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In standard homodyne detection, an unknown optical signal state is combined with a local oscillator (LO) at a beam splitter (BS), and the appropriately integrated intensities at the two outputs of the BS are subtracted to obtain an approximate quadrature measurement. Accurate quadrature measurements require a high-amplitude LO and high-efficiency detectors. By varying the LO phase, the optical state in the mode matching the LO can be reconstructed. In many practical settings, a high-intensity LO is not desirable, which has led to the exploration of homodyne-like measurement schemes that replace photodiodes with photon-counting detectors and use an LO with a low average photon number. In this paper, we investigate what features of the unknown state can be determined by counting the total-photon number in one or both outgoing paths after the BS, keeping the mode of the LO fixed but adjusting its phase and magnitude. We find that it is possible to determine the unknown optical state in the mode matching the LO conditional on the number of photons in the orthogonal modes on the same path. If both the phase and the intensity of the LO can be varied, the statistics of just one of the detectors is sufficient to infer these parameters, but both detectors are required when the LO has a fixed intensity. Our analysis is carried out in the infinite-data limit, assuming access to the full photon-number distribution from one or both detectors.

</details>


### [70] [Quantum Mpemba effect in a driven-dissipative atom-waveguide coupled system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23971)
*Hao-Kai Chi,H. H. Jen*

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子 Mpemba 效应（QME）——扰动更强的系统反而更快弛豫到稳态——已在多种开放量子体系中受到关注，但其与耗散模式空间结构（尤其方向性耗散与超/亚辐射模式的局域化）之间的关系尚不清楚，本文旨在探究波导 QED 体系中集体衰减模式的空间分布如何调控 QME。

Method: 研究对象为弱驱动耗散原子阵列耦合到一维手性波导。系统从稳态出发，将单个原子重置到基态作为局域淬火，追踪随后的弛豫动力学；通过对比不同淬火位置（边界、体内、界面附近）以及不同方向性衰减强度，分析超辐射与亚辐射集体模式的空间分布对早期与晚期弛豫速率的贡献。还将两个具有不同粒子间距的原子阵列耦合，构造界面结构。

Result: 淬火靠近阵列边界的原子时，由于与超辐射模式耦合增强并向波导直接辐射光子，初始弛豫更快，表现为边缘增强 QME；强方向性衰减促使超辐射模式在偏好边界附近空间累积，进一步放大该效应，形成趋肤增强 QME。晚期弛豫主要由局域于阵列体内的长寿命亚辐射模式主导。在双阵列界面体系中，淬火界面附近原子因同时较弱地参与超辐射与亚辐射模式，早期弛豫更慢但晚期更快趋近稳态，即界面诱导 QME。

Conclusion: 集体衰减模式的空间结构与方向性耗散共同决定了反常量子弛豫行为，QME 的强弱与快慢可由淬火位置与模式局域化的匹配程度加以调控；这为波导 QED 体系中加速稳态制备提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The quantum Mpemba effect (QME) describes the counterintuitive phenomenon in which a more strongly perturbed quantum system relaxes toward its steady state faster than a less perturbed one. Here, we investigate the QME in a weakly driven-dissipative atomic array coupled to a one-dimensional chiral waveguide. Starting from the steady state, we introduce a local quench by resetting a single atom to its ground state and examine the subsequent relaxation dynamics. We find that quenching atoms near the array boundaries induces faster initial relaxation through enhanced coupling to superradiant modes and direct photon emission into the waveguide, giving rise to an edge-enhanced QME. Strong directional decay further amplifies this effect by promoting the spatial accumulation of superradiant modes near the preferred boundary, leading to a skin-enhanced QME. At later times, relaxation is increasingly governed by long-lived subradiant modes localized predominantly in the bulk of the array. By coupling two atomic arrays with different interparticle spacings, we further demonstrate an interface-induced QME, where quenching an atom near the interface makes the system relax more slowly at early times but approach its steady state faster at later times, owing to weak participation in both superradiant and subradiant modes. Our findings reveal how the spatial structure of collective decay modes and directional dissipation jointly govern anomalous quantum relaxation, offering potential routes toward accelerated steady-state preparation in waveguide QED systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米格点中增益、损耗与非互易耦合的组合可产生指数增长的方向性放大，但其在任意维度下是否具有普适的拓扑起源尚不清楚。

Method: 引入amoeba概念（响应生成函数奇点的对数像），考察对数参数空间中amoeba补集的连通分支，每支携带离散的拓扑标号；并分析渐近响应由延伸至负无穷的分支所支配。

Result: 渐近响应由指向负无穷的补集分支决定；当对数参数空间原点位于该分支之外且不在其边界上时，必存在某些方向呈指数放大；否则任何方向均无方向性放大。

Conclusion: 给出任意维度非厄米格点响应分类的一般拓扑判据，实现维度无关的方向性放大拓扑判定。

Abstract: In non-Hermitian lattices, combinations of gain, loss and nonreciprocal couplings can generate exponentially growing directional amplification with applications in signal processing and precision measurement. An intriguing question is whether such amplification has a universal topological origin in arbitrary dimensions. Here we establish a dimension-independent framework to uncover the topological origin of directional amplification based on the concept of amoeba, which is the logarithmic image of the singularities of the response generating function. The complement of the amoeba in the logarithmic parameter space consists of connected components, each carrying a distinct and discrete topological label. We show that the asymptotic response is governed by the component extending toward negative infinity. When the origin of the logarithmic parameter space is outside this component and not on its boundary, there must exist some directions in which the response is exponentially amplified. Otherwise, there is no directional amplification in any direction. Our results provide a general topological criterion for classifying the responses in arbitrary-dimensional non-Hermitian lattices.

</details>

*Hao Zhu,Guo-Feng Zhang,Wen-Long Ma*

TL;DR: 提出基于测量记录统计的(K+1)维特征向量框架，用于高效识别量子系统的守恒扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 守恒律将希尔伯特空间分割为不变扇区，但这些扇区难以直接识别；辅助探针的重复测量记录蕴含扇区信息，需要一套系统方法加以利用。

Conclusion: 该框架为守恒扇区识别提供了高效途径，并表明时间关联对区分扇区内部动力学具有重要作用。

Abstract: Conservation laws partition the Hilbert space of a quantum system into invariant sectors that are often difficult to identify directly. Repeated measurements on an ancillary probe, however, generate a measurement record that provides information about the occupied sector. Here we develop a framework for identifying conserved sectors using a $(K+1)$-dimensional feature vector of record statistics, comprising the average outcome frequency and pair-outcome correlations up to $K$ measurement cycles apart. For fixed $K$, the feature vector can be evaluated at a cost linear in record length. In quantum non-demolition measurement models, the average outcome frequency suffices for accurate sector identification. For an interacting spin bath with nontrivial dynamics within each sector, incorporating temporal correlations between successive and more widely separated measurements into the record feature vector substantially improves classification fidelity. Our framework provides an efficient approach to conserved-sector identification and demonstrates the importance of temporal correlations in distinguishing the underlying sector dynamics.

</details>


### [73] [Vanilla Adiabatic Quantum Optimization Can Have Subexponential Advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24035)
*Yufan Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造了一类目标函数，使标准绝热量子优化（横场为-Xi之和）在poly(n)时间内找到全局最小值，而黑箱经典算法需exp(n^Ω(1))时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Leng等人的工作利用横场Ising混合器实现了类似亚指数量子加速，但其目标函数构造依赖线性化技术。本文希望说明：标准（vanilla）绝热演化——即仅使用简单横向场 \sum Xi 而非特殊设计的Ising混合器——是否也能获得超越经典的加速。

Method: 构造一族目标函数 f:{0,1}^n→R，沿哈密顿量 H(s)=-(1-s)\sum_i X_i + s\sum_x f(x)|x><x| 进行绝热演化；关键技术是用新方法替换前人工作中的线性化技术以适应该简单混合器。

Result: 在该构造下，vanilla绝热量子优化可在poly(n)时间内找到全局最小解，而任何黑箱经典算法需exp(n^{Ω(1)})时间，即给出多项式对指数级的量子-经典分离。

Conclusion: 标准绝热量子优化（无需定制混合器）即可实现指数级黑箱量子加速，扩展了Leng等人的亚指数结果。

Abstract: We construct a family of objective functions $f \colon \{0,1\}^n \to \mathbb{R}$ for which vanilla adiabatic quantum optimization along $H(s) = -(1-s)\sum_{i=1}^n X_i + s\sum_x f(x)\lvert x\rangle\!\langle x\rvert$ finds the global minimizer in $\operatorname{poly}(n)$ time, whereas any classical algorithm requires $\exp(n^{Ω(1)})$ time to do so in the black-box model. Our result is achieved by replacing the linearization technique in an earlier work of Leng et al. (arXiv:2504.14841), which establishes a similar subexponential quantum speedup using a transverse field Ising mixer rather than $-\sum_{i=1}^n X_i$.

</details>


### [74] [Accelerating Quantum Error Correction Simulations under Coherent Noise with Compiled Clifford-Augmented Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24044)

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 编译型CAMPS框架通过复用Clifford变换、回收测量释放的量子比特、压缩残余态演化及采样编译加速，高效模拟相干错误下的量子纠错逻辑性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估量子纠错需理解相干错误对逻辑性能的影响；张量网络虽能自然刻画相干干涉，但纠缠增长和大比特数使重复采样代价高昂。

Method: 提出编译型Clifford增广矩阵乘积态（CAMPS）框架：跨shot复用Clifford变换、回收测量释放的比特、演化压缩残余态，并针对MPS布局做旋转融合与编译以加速采样。

Conclusion: 编译型CAMPS为研究更大容错电路中的相干错误传播提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Reliable assessment of quantum error correction requires understanding how coherent errors affect logical performance. Although tensor networks naturally capture coherent interference, entanglement growth and large qubit counts make repeated sampling costly. Here we develop a compiled Clifford-Augmented Matrix Product State (CAMPS) framework that reuses Clifford transformations across shots, recycles qubits released by measurements, evolves a compressed residual state, and accelerates sampling through rotation fusion and compilation tailored to the MPS layout. Surface-code benchmarks under fixed-sign coherent noise yield accurate logical error estimates at small bond dimensions, while random-sign simulations up to distance $d=9$ and on the $[[72,12,6]]$ bivariate bicycle code match the Pauli-twirling approximation results. Compared with uncompiled CAMPS, compiled CAMPS converges at smaller bond dimensions and achieves order-of-magnitude sampling speedups at equal bond dimension, opening a scalable route to studying coherent error propagation in larger fault-tolerant circuits.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 奇偶链长下非衰减振荡模式的存在性不同，导致弛豫行为定性差异；Liouvillian出现非零纯虚本征值；在乘积态制备后，键关联或磁化存在持续贡献；该机制在固定边界耗散规则下可推广至偶数程cluster相互作用；持续模式的存在性与频率不依赖于正边界耗散率的大小。

Conclusion: 链长奇偶性通过体Majorana路径—边界跳跃算符耦合控制持续动力学的存在性，为开放量子链中持久振荡模式的判据与设计提供了结构性解释。

Abstract: We investigate odd-even effects on Liouvillian spectra and relaxation dynamics in boundary-driven XY and cluster chains. With the bulk couplings and boundary dissipation rule fixed, the parity of chain length determines whether non-decaying oscillatory modes exist. Consequently, odd- and even-sized chains exhibit qualitatively different dynamical behaviors. We trace this mechanism to the coupling between bulk Majorana paths and the boundary jump operators. An isolated path supports undamped modes, and products of an even number of its Majorana operators span a non?decaying subspace of even operators. This structure yields nonzero purely imaginary eigenvalues of the Liouvillian and persistent contributions to bond correlations or magnetization after preparation in product states. The mechanism extends to cluster interactions of even range under a fixed boundary dissipation rule. The existence and frequencies of the persistent modes are independent of the magnitudes of the positive boundary dissipation rates. Our results unveil how chain parity controls the existence of persistent dynamics.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum information can be reliably transmitted at rates below the quantum capacity with an error that vanishes as the number of channel uses grows. The reliability function characterizes the optimal exponential decay rate of this error. For quantum erasure channels with a prime input dimension, we determine the reliability function exactly at all positive communication rates below capacity that meet or exceed an explicit critical rate. Notably, stabilizer coding strategies attain this optimal reliability throughout this regime.

</details>

Abstract: We present a protocol for implementing a controlled-controlled-Z (CCZ) gate on a neutral-atom platform, employing three individually addressable atoms confined in a linear array of optical tweezers and operating under finite Rydberg-Rydberg interaction (RRI). The protocol combines successive excitation of the Rydberg atoms with analytically determined pulses and uses two Rydberg levels to access opposite-sign interaction shifts. We show two different paths to get the conditional phase in the target atom: a pair of target pulses with opposite phases and a single detuned target pulse followed by a local phase correction. We derive analytical expressions for the time-integrated Rydberg populations to calculate the associated decay errors and also evaluate gate performance through master-equation simulations. Numerical comparisons with a fast Rydberg-antiblockade (RAB)-based CCZ protocol show improved gate performance at larger interatomic separations within the parameter regime considered. Monte Carlo sampling further quantifies the sensitivity to thermally induced position fluctuations. These results identify the tradeoffs between gate duration, Rydberg-state exposure, and interaction sensitivity, providing an analytically specified route to native three-qubit phase gates beyond the strong-blockade regime.

</details>


### [78] [Quantum Parallel Algorithm for Multivariate Trace Estimation with Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24147)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元迹Tr(A1A2…Ak)估计在多个领域是基础任务，但现有方法电路深度随矩阵个数k增长，难以在近期含噪声量子设备上实现。

Method: 以制备矩阵向量化态的方式访问矩阵；由此得到多元迹的张量网络表示，据此设计并行算法。电路深度仅由输入oracle深度主导、与k无关，代价是量子比特数Θ(k)；电路具模块化结构，可显式分布到O(k)个设备上。另给出多类矩阵的高效制备电路。

Result: 电路深度与k无关、比特数为Θ(k)，形成深度—规模权衡；可分布式实现。应用于谱和估计与经典Lanczos方法的量子实现，将问题归约为多元迹之和，避免构造矩阵多项式变换，实现两级并行；对特定矩阵类，电路深度降低的倍数等于逼近多项式次数。

Conclusion: 浅层深度与可分布式实现使该算法特别适合近期量子设备，并具较广的矩阵类适用性。

Abstract: Estimating multivariate traces Tr(A1A2......Ak) is a fundamental task arising across diverse fields. In this paper, We develop a quantum algorithm for this problem tailored to near-term quantum devices. We access matrices by preparing their vectorized states. This access mode yields a tensor-network representation of the multivariate trace, which in turn guides the design of a parallel algorithm. The resulting circuit has depth dominated solely by that of the input oracle and independent of the number of matrices k, at the cost of a qubit count scaling as Θ(k)-a trade-off between circuit depth and circuit size. Moreover, the circuit exhibits a simple modular structure that allows explicit distribution over O(k) devices. We further establish efficient preparation circuits for a broad range of matrix classes, demonstrating the wide applicability of our approach. we present two applications: spectral sum estimation and a quantum implementation of the classical Lanczos method. Both reduce the target problem to sums of multivariate traces, thereby avoiding the construction of matrix polynomial transformations and achieving two levels of parallelism. For certain matrix classes, the resulting algorithms reduce circuit depth by a factor equal to the degree of the approximating polynomial. The shallow circuit depth and distributed implementation make our algorithms particularly suitable for near-term devices.

</details>


### [79] [Gradient Blindness in Adaptive Architecture Search for IQP Born Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24174)
*Sebastian Wölckert,Gerhard Stenzel,Michael Poppel,Claudia Linnhoff-Popien,Jonas Stein*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究IQP Born machine自适应生长算法中"种子电路"的瓶颈：候选门梯度是否非零完全由生成元的奇偶线性无关性决定，据此设计能遍历全部候选的种子（单比特门+Chow-Liu树边），在12/16比特上用10–13%参数匹配全电路，484比特下以6%参数超越稠密两体电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应生长算法每轮只添加损失梯度最大的候选门，梯度为零的门永远不会被加入，因此初始种子电路就决定了可达的电路子空间。此前工作未刻画"哪些候选门梯度必然为零"这一结构性限制，导致常用单比特门种子可能系统性地遗漏某些门。

Method: (1) 理论证明：候选门的梯度在某些角度下非零，当且仅当其生成元等于电路中已有生成元的奇数个线性无关生成元之和（奇偶/线性代数判据）。(2) 由此推出单比特门种子的局限：它永远无法添加作用于偶数个比特的门。(3) 提出新种子=单比特门+Chow-Liu树的边；在484比特规模引入heredity pool限制高阶门的搜索空间。实验在12、16比特上做精确梯度验证与参数压缩对比，并与稠密两体电路比较测试损失。

Result: 12比特精确梯度实验证实单比特种子从不添加偶数比特门；单比特门+Chow-Liu树边种子可"看见"所有候选，其生长电路与全电路在12和16比特上效果匹配，参数仅用10–13%；在484比特下，基于该种子的heredity pool生长电路以约6%参数取得比稠密两体电路更低的测试损失，但增加高阶门并无额外收益。

Conclusion: 自适应生长算法的可达性由种子的生成元代数的奇偶结构决定；选择与数据依赖结构对齐的种子（Chow-Liu树边）可在保持表达能力的同时大幅压缩参数，且两体门已足够，高阶门非必要。

Abstract: Instantaneous quantum polynomial (IQP) Born machines learn a probability distribution from data with a circuit of commuting gates that a classical computer trains at hundreds of qubits. Adaptive algorithms grow a sparse circuit from a small seed by adding in every round the candidate gates with the largest loss gradient. Such algorithms never add a candidate whose gradient vanishes, so the choice of seed decides which circuits they can reach. Here we show that a candidate gate has a nonzero gradient for some angles of the circuit exactly when its generator is the sum of an odd number of linearly independent generators already in the circuit. The common seed of one-qubit gates therefore never adds a gate on an even number of qubits, which exact gradients on 12 qubits confirm. A seed of one-qubit gates plus the edges of the Chow-Liu tree sees every candidate, and its circuits match the full circuits on 12 and 16 qubits with 10 to 13 percent of their parameters. At 484 qubits, a heredity pool keeps the search over higher-order gates feasible, and the circuits grown from this seed reach a lower test loss than the dense two-body circuit with 6 percent of its parameters, although the higher-order gates add no gain.

</details>
