Motivation: 量子存储器可显著提升量子网络性能，但其复杂度高、在通信波段性能受限，阻碍了实际部署；现有存储器辅助MDI-QKD多依赖双存储器架构，硬件开销大。

Method: 利用原子系综存储器中静止物质量子比特与飞行光子量子比特在单个量子存储器内部发生干涉，构建存储器辅助MDI-QKD协议；定量对比无存储器MDI-QKD与现有双存储器架构，并给出与ORCA（非共振级联吸收）存储器能力兼容的具体设计方案。

Result: 在相同条件下获得更优的成钥率，并在明显更短的距离上超越无存储器界限；同时将存储器与探测器数量各减半，显著降低硬件需求。

Conclusion: 存储器内干涉为近期可实现的存储器增强型量子安全通信提供了一条高性能、资源高效的路径，ORCA存储器是尤其有前景的实现平台。

Abstract: Quantum memories offer to significantly extend the performance of quantum networks, but their complexity and limited performance at telecommunication wavelengths have so far hindered practical deployment. We propose a new memory-assisted measurement device independent quantum key distribution (MDI-QKD) protocol based on in-memory interference. Our approach leverages progress in atomic ensemble memories that enable the interference of a stationary matter qubit and a travelling photonic qubit inside a single quantum memory. We quantitatively evaluate the performance of this scheme against memoryless MDI-QKD and existing two-memory architectures, showing that it not only achieves superior secret key rates and surpasses the memoryless bound at substantially shorter distances, but also reduces hardware requirements by halving the number of memories and detectors. Our results further indicate that off resonant cascaded absorption (ORCA) memories are a particularly promising platform for implementing this protocol, and we outline a concrete design compatible with their capabilities. These findings demonstrate that in-memory interference provides a higher performance, and resource efficient route toward near term memory enhanced quantum secure communications.

Conclusion: 虚性资源的定量刻画需超越输入鲁棒性R与最大条件鲁棒性κ，面积度量R²κ提供了忠实、凸、平均单调且可计算界定的刻画，并可区分在(R, κ)意义下等价的态之间的实操作可转换性。

Abstract: Two qubit states with the same robustness of imaginarity can differ in the robustness attainable by real filtering on a successful outcome. We represent the input robustness $R$ and the largest conditional robustness $κ$ by the base of a right triangle and the slope of its hypotenuse, respectively. Twice the area, $R^2κ$, defines a faithful, convex measure that is monotone on average under real instruments. In arbitrary finite dimensions, maximizing the mean normalized area of the extracted qubits gives the pointwise smallest strongly monotone extension of the qubit measure. We derive computable bounds on this area by determining the maximum mean squared robustness of the output qubits. This maximum equals the Bures quantum $χ^2$ divergence between the input and its real part. A projection onto orthogonal real subspaces attains the maximum and, when its outcome is retained, preserves the quantum Fisher information at the midpoint of a mixture of the input and its complex conjugate. We obtain exact areas for states with commuting real and imaginary parts and for pure states mixed with white noise. Two eight-dimensional states have identical $R$, $κ$, and optimal mean squared robustness, yet their different areas rule out deterministic conversion by real operations in one direction.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究“虚性”（imaginarity）的亲和度平方度量：给出纯态的闭式表达（由共轭重叠模长决定），证明其凸屋顶扩展是忠实的、凸的、在实操作下单调且在选择实操作下平均不增，并对单量子比特混合态精确求解凸屋顶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子虚性刻画量子态中无法在固定参考基下用实密度矩阵表示的部分，需要一个便于计算且具操作性意义的定量度量，并厘清其与相干性度量的关系。

Method: 对纯态，在实态凸集上最小化亲和度平方度量，得到闭式表达，其值由态与其复共轭之间共轭重叠的模长决定；再取凸屋顶扩展至混合态，并验证忠实性、凸性、在实操作（乃至选择实操作）下的单调性；对单量子比特混合态精确计算凸屋顶；并与辅助的亲和度平方相干泛函作对比、给出相应相位权重。

Result: 纯态度量有闭式解，并在且仅在态至多相差全局相位为实时取零；凸屋顶扩展是忠实、凸的，在实操作下单调，在选择实操作下平均不增；单量子比特混合态的凸屋顶可精确表示为非对角密度矩阵元虚部的函数。

Conclusion: 亲和度平方虚性是一种合法且可解析计算的虚性度量，其单比特情形可显式给出，并可通过相位权重与相干性泛函建立联系。

Abstract: Quantum imaginarity characterizes the part of a quantum state that cannot be represented by real density matrices in a fixed reference basis. We study an affinity-square measure of imaginarity for pure states and its convex-roof extension to mixed states. The pure state quantity is defined by minimizing the affinity-square measure over the convex set of real states. This optimization admits a closed expression determined by the modulus of the conjugation overlap between a state and its complex conjugate. The measure vanishes precisely when the state is real up to a global phase. We prove that the convex-roof extension is faithful, convex, monotone under real operations, and nonincreasing on average under selective real operations. For arbitrary single-qubit mixed states, we evaluate the convex roof exactly and express it in terms of the imaginary component of the off-diagonal density-matrix element. We also compare pure-state imaginarity with an auxiliary affinity-square coherence functional and give the corresponding phase weights.

</details>


### [83] [QuFrame: Pauli Frame Tracking for Fault-Tolerant Quantum Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24328)
*Aleksandra Świerkowska,Ilgaz Er,Emmanouil Giortamis,Pramod Bhatotia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QuFrame——一种在1μs内闭环量子纠错的控制微架构，通过QuFrameISA解耦指令执行与Pauli框架管理，并由异步主解码器+片上回退解码器（144ns）保证实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用量子计算中非Clifford门（魔态注入）引入对纠错测量的硬实时控制流依赖，控制器必须在严格1μs周期内完成syndrome提取、解码与Pauli框架更新；而已有Pauli框架跟踪方案多为理论层面，缺乏对分布式控制流水线中跟踪、解析与同步更新等微架构瓶颈的研究。

Method: 1) QuFrameISA：将指令执行与持续的Pauli框架管理干净解耦；2) 每量子比特的指令解析引擎（IRE），通过单次查表完成指令与框架的解析，实现分布式执行；3) Pauli框架管理单元（Pauli Frame Management Unit），支持独立、无锁的并发提交；4) 异步主解码器配合片上回退解码器（144ns内解析），在硬件层面原生调和冲突更新。

Result: QuFrame可在1μs内闭环QEC并维持QPU速率吞吐；回退解码器解析延迟为144ns；硬件原生调和分歧更新且不降低逻辑错误率。

Conclusion: 面向实时Pauli框架跟踪的控制微架构设计可行，指令集层面的解耦加主/回退解码器混合方案能在严格时限下满足通用容错量子计算的实时控制需求。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires continuous quantum error correction (QEC) to suppress physical noise. While Clifford-only workloads can defer corrections to software post-processing, universal computation breaks this paradigm: executing non-Clifford gates via magic-state gadgets introduces hard, real-time control-flow dependencies on error-corrected measurements. Consequently, the controller must extract syndromes, decode them, and update the Pauli frame in real time, closing the entire QEC loop within a strict $1\,μ\text{s}$ cycle. However, prior proposals on Pauli frame tracking remain largely theoretical, leaving the microarchitectural bottlenecks of tracking, resolving, and synchronizing frame updates across distributed control pipelines unaddressed.
  To this end, we present QuFrame, a control microarchitecture for real-time Pauli frame tracking that closes the QEC loop within $1\,μ\text{s}$ while sustaining QPU-rate throughput. By introducing QuFrameISA, we cleanly decouple instruction execution from continuous Pauli frame management. Execution is distributed across per-qubit Instruction Resolution Engines (IREs) that resolve instructions against the frame via a single table lookup, while a Pauli Frame Management Unit enables independent, lock-free concurrent commits. To strictly guarantee the $1\,μ\text{s}$ deadline, QuFrame pairs an asynchronous primary decoder with an on-chip fallback decoder that resolves in $144\,\text{ns}$, natively reconciling divergent updates in hardware without degrading logical error rates.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间仓纠缠与电信光纤网兼容，适合星地QKD，但其相位敏感测量易受卫星运动引起的多普勒效应破坏，需要在双站独立多普勒频移条件下保持相位基关联。

Method: 建立谱-时分析框架：星载SPDC源、两条独立下行链路、各站UMZI（400 ps延迟匹配）执行BBM92；量化每链路时间仓间隔变化与载波相位漂移；采用零多普勒标定将相位分解为信号/闲频链路独立贡献，实现本地修正而无需站间实时相位交换；提出星历辅助前馈架构——辅助干涉仪测量同传播经典见证场重建本地多普勒相位，由相位调制器在量子信道上补偿（1 Hz采样保持）。

Result: 模拟LEO过境中每链路时间仓间隔变化至多8.8 fs，合成载波相移约38 rad，主要恶化相位基关联。无噪声模型下，1 Hz采样保持校正使相位基QBER低于2%，保留93%理想渐近密钥率；累积18次过境后校正的有限密钥长度达理想值95%，而仅依据星历重标宇称、不做光学校正仅约8%。

Conclusion: 多普勒主要损伤相位基，通过零多普勒标定分离链路相位并配合星历辅助前馈光学校正，可在无需站间实时相位交换的条件下有效恢复相位基关联与密钥产出。

Abstract: Time-bin entanglement is promising for satellite Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) due to its compatibility with telecom fiber networks, but its phase-sensitive measurements are vulnerable to Doppler effects induced by satellite motion. We present a spectral-temporal framework for time-bin-entangled photon pairs generated onboard a satellite via Spontaneous Parametric Down Conversion (SPDC) and distributed to two Optical Ground Stations (OGSs) over independent downlinks with distinct Doppler shifts. Each OGS is equipped with an Unbalanced Mach-Zehnder Interferometer (UMZI) used to perform Bennett-Brassard-Mermin 1992 (BBM92) entangled-photon QKD. In a simulated Low Earth Orbit (LEO) pass with matched 400 ps analyzer delays, the received time-bin separation changes by at most 8.8 fs per link, while the combined carrier phase shifts by about 38 rad, which primarily degrades phase-basis correlations. After zero-Doppler calibration, this phase separates into independent signal-and idler-link contributions, allowing each station to correct its link Doppler phase shift locally, without real-time phase exchange between the stations. We propose an ephemeris-assisted feed-forward architecture, in which an auxiliary interferometer analyzes a co-propagating classical witness field to reconstruct the local Doppler-induced phase, which a phase modulator then compensates on the quantum channel. In a noise-free model, a 1 Hz sample-and-hold correction rate keeps the phase-basis Quantum Bit Error Rate (QBER) below 2% and retains 93% of the ideal asymptotic key yield. After 18 accumulated passes, the corrected finite secret key reaches 95% of its ideal length, whereas relabeling the parity from the ephemeris without optical correction reaches only about 8%.

</details>


### [85] [Long-distance continuous variable entanglement distribution over deployed fibre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24411)
*Huy Q. Nguyen,Benjamin L. Larsen,Luiz C. C. P. Filho,Yijian Meng,Mikael Ø. Lassen,Jonas S. Neergaard-Nielsen,Tobias Gehring,Ulrik L. Andersen,Adnan A. E. Hajomer*

TL;DR: 首次在实际部署的城市光纤（23 km 与 450 m 链路）上，利用独立本振激光与偏振分集相干接收机完成连续变量纠缠分发，并用数字方式恢复时延、相位与偏振，无需光学稳相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CV 纠缠可确定性产生、室温相干探测，适合量子网络，但实际光纤中远端接收端的光学相位、偏振与时间同步稳定化难题阻碍了其外场部署。

Method: 制备双模压缩真空态，两模经 23 km（约 7 dB 损耗）和 450 m（约 1 dB 损耗）现网光纤送至远端节点；各节点使用自由运转激光器与偏振分集相干接收机；同传导频音实现时延、相位与偏振的数字恢复，模间关联用于确定正交分量对齐。

Result: Simon-Duan 判据值 1.915±0.013，低于可分离界 2；对数负度为 0.067±0.011，纠缠得到认证。

Conclusion: 以数字重建替代光学稳定化，为可扩展 CV 量子网络提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Continuous-variable (CV) entanglement offers deterministic generation and room-temperature coherent detection for quantum networks, but distributing it over deployed fibre has been hindered by the need to stabilise optical phase, polarisation, and timing at remote receivers. Here we demonstrate, to our knowledge, the first distribution of CV entanglement over deployed metropolitan fibre using independent coherent receivers. The two modes of a two-mode squeezed vacuum state are delivered to remote nodes through 23 km ($\approx 7$ dB loss) and 450 m ($\approx 1$ dB loss) deployed fibre links. Each node uses its own free-running laser and polarisation-diverse coherent receiver. Co-propagating pilot tones enable digital recovery of timing, phase and polarisation, while inter-mode correlations fix the quadrature alignment. We certify entanglement with a Simon-Duan value of $1.915\pm 0.013$, below the separability bound of 2 and a logarithmic negativity of $0.067\pm0.011$. By replacing optical stabilisation with digital reconstruction, our approach offers a practical route towards scalable CV quantum networks.

</details>

*Vojtěch Novák,Radovan Rečka,Ondřej Knebl,Ivan Zelinka*

Motivation: 经典线路模拟支撑量子软件开发与验证，并为评估量子优势提供基线；不同模拟表示、数值精度与并行资源分配对完整能量评估成本的影响缺乏系统对比指导。

Result: 在所测实现中，直接Pauli演化更适合复杂分子VHA，GPU在更宽的自旋与硬件高效线路上加速明显；MPS在纠缠受限可精确压缩时有效；经修正的CPU FP32可达到测试容差但成本高于FP64。两层全LiH的NumPy匹配Pauli更新提速3.45倍；28量子比特四层TFIM上GPU相对18线程Aer在FP64下约提速30倍。

Conclusion: 负载规模、启动开销、worker内存及任务特定剖析都会改变最优资源分配；论文提出联合选择表示、精度与worker的流程，在精度、内存与核心约束下比较完整能量评估的代价。

Abstract: Classical circuit simulation supports quantum software development and verification and provides a baseline for assessing quantum advantage. We compare gate-based statevector simulation, direct Pauli evolution and matrix product states for molecular and spin Variational Hamiltonian Ansatze and hardware-efficient ansatze on CPU/GPU systems, varying width, depth, precision and resource allocation. In the tested implementations, direct Pauli evolution benefits complex molecular VHA, whereas GPUs accelerate wider spin and hardware efficient circuits. Matrix product state is useful when limited entanglement permits accurate compression; corrected CPU FP32 can meet the tested tolerance while costing more than FP64. Workload size, startup, worker memory and task-specific profiling can change the preferred allocation. Matched NumPy Pauli updates accelerate two-layer full LiH by 3.45-fold; the GPU gives approximately 30-fold acceleration over 18-thread Aer for 28-qubit, four-layer TFIM, both in FP64. We provide a procedure for choosing representation, precision and worker allocation jointly, comparing the cost of complete energy evaluations under accuracy, memory and core constraints.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上TDHO的精确解依赖Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量方法，该方法虽有效但缺乏与Lie对称性结构的清晰联系；作者希望从等价变换与Lie点对称的角度给出统一、更本质的刻画。

Method: 采用等价变换与Lie点对称分析：构造含七个参数的最一般点变换，实现TDHO与常谐振子之间的相互映射；利用SL(2,R)与规范群构成的特定子群构造不变量解；并考察LR算子在SL(2,R)不变解族上的作用。

Result: 得到七参数点变换族；由此副产品重新导出常谐振子的Schrödinger传播子(Mehler核)；在不用LR不变量的情况下，通过Lie代数方法重构TDHO的归一化波函数；并证明LR算子在相关SL(2,R)不变解族上定义相容的二阶微分约束。

Conclusion: 等价变换与Lie点对称方法为TDHO提供了不依赖LR不变量的系统求解途径，并澄清了Lewis-Riesenfeld算子与TDHO的Lie对称结构之间的关系，即其可视为相容的二阶微分约束。

Abstract: We revisit the time-dependent harmonic oscillator (TDHO) in quantum mechanics based on the notion of equivalence transformations and Lie point symmetries. Within this framework, we give the most general point transformations depending on seven parameters which map from the TDHO to the usual harmonic oscillator (HO) and the other way around. As a byproduct we reconstruct the Schrödinger propagator (Mehler's kernel) for the time-independent harmonic oscillator. We demonstrate, without recourse to the Lewis--Riesenwert (LR) invariant, how Lie algebraic methods enable alternative derivation of the normalized wave functions of the TDHO by constructing solutions invariant under some specific subgroup of $\SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ combined with the gauge group. We further show that, on the relevant $\SL(2,\mathbb{R})$-invariant solution family, the Lewis--Riesenfeld operator defines a compatible second-order differential constraint, thereby clarifying its relation to the Lie symmetry structure of the TDHO.

</details>

*Arthur Charrier,Timoth{é}e Guerra,Nicolas Baier,Julie Abergel,Antoine Coquiard,Amaury Mavel,Sebastien Renet,Frederic Berger,Aur{é}lie Vandeneynde,Sandy Brunet-Manquat,Jean-Alain Nicolas,Jean-Luc Moro,Jean-Marc Merolla,Johan Rothman*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The performance of HgCdTe APDs for single photon detection at T=80 K has been characterized using a hybrized Resistive Trans-Impedance Amplifier (RTIA) and a deported Capacitance Trans-impedance amplifier (CTIA). The single-photon detection capacity was characterized with the HgCdTe APDs operated in linear mode with high APD gain, M>400, and hybridized with the RTIA. The measurements revealed an external photon detection efficiency (PDE) of 42 % to 55 %, and a dark count rate (DCR) of 2.6 MHz to 17 MHz, for an amplitude threshold ranging from 2.5 to 4.5 times the rms noise of the amplifier. Taking into account the unity gain quantum efficiency, QE=63%, this corresponds to an internal PDE ranging from 66 % to 86 %. Using the deported CTIA, it was demonstrated that the high level of DCR is not the intrinsic limit of the APD technology. The CTIA approach enables direct measurement of the APD dark current, Idark, with gain-normalized dark current levels as low as Idark/M=10.9 fA at M = 450. This corresponds to a DCR of 150 Hz which is compatible with the requirements for most of the quantum photonics applications.

</details>


### [89] [Bound entanglement as a trigonometric moment problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24473)
*Lilong Qian,Teng Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在多量子比特赤道自旋相干态乘积的实权重混合类中，将PPT纠缠态的基本问题（归属、白噪声鲁棒性、最小秩）转化为截断三角矩问题，并给出若干精确解答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPT纠缠态是 entanglement 简明描述的主要障碍，需要在一类可解析处理的多体态族中系统研究其结构、鲁棒性与秩。

Method: 考虑若干量子比特组的赤道自旋相干态之积的混合（实但可负权重），将问题化为截断三角矩问题；全可分性等价于关联多层Toeplitz矩阵为环面上正测度的矩矩阵。

Result: 三量子比特情形：所有Lorentz不变量非零的四秩PPT纠缠态经可逆局域操作属于该类，验证了Garberg–Irgens–Myrheim的假设并给出该七维族的完备不变量；不变量为零时仅一参数族属于该类。对由不可扩展乘积基构造的三量子比特态，全可分白噪声阈值λ*=1-2/(1+√(1+s))（s∈(0,1]为局域酉不变量），Shifts基为λ*=3-2√2；8个局域Pauli设置的闭式见证可探测该族全部纠缠态。两组情形构造最小秩极值态，三组情形在多种配置下确定族内纠缠最小秩。

Conclusion: 矩问题框架为该类态提供精确可解的描述，统一刻画PPT纠缠态的归属、噪声鲁棒性与最小秩，并给出实用纠缠见证。

Abstract: Entangled states that are positive under partial transposition (PPT) are the main obstruction to a simple description of entanglement. We study three basic questions about them within a class of multiqubit states, the mixtures, with real but possibly negative weights, of products of equatorial spin coherent states of several groups of qubits: which PPT entangled states belong to the class, how robust their entanglement is to white noise, and how small their rank can be. In this class each question becomes a question about a truncated trigonometric moment problem. Every such state is PPT across every bipartition, and it is fully separable exactly when an associated multi-level Toeplitz matrix is the moment matrix of a positive measure on the torus. We answer the three questions exactly in the following cases. For three qubits, every rank-four PPT entangled state with nonzero Lorentz invariant belongs to the class after invertible local operators, which proves a working hypothesis of Garberg, Irgens and Myrheim and yields a complete set of invariants for this seven-dimensional family; among the states with vanishing invariant, exactly a one-parameter family belongs to it. For every three-qubit state built from an unextendible product basis, the white-noise threshold for full separability is $λ^*=1-2/(1+\sqrt{1+s})$ with a local-unitary invariant $s\in(0,1]$, and $λ^*=3-2\sqrt2$ for the Shifts basis; a witness in closed form detects every entangled state of this family with eight local Pauli settings. For two groups of qubits we construct extremal states of minimal rank, and for three groups we determine the minimal rank of entanglement within the class in several configurations.

</details>


### [90] [From BBM92 Measurements to Secure Keys: Challenges in a Practical Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24478)
*Karolina P. Schatz,Elia Falcioni,Panagiotis Papanastasiou,Marco Lucamarini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文考察BBM92协议的两种实现（常规无源接收机与减半探测器数目的时间复用结构），表征其无源偏振分析器，并给出基于连续泵浦SPDC源与阈值探测器的实验结果，讨论后处理中难以满足现有安全性证明假设的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BBM92是重要且广泛采用的QKD协议，其实际安全性依赖于理论安全证明所设前提是否成立；实践中实现方式与证明假设之间存在差距，需要实证检验。

Method: 对比分析常规无源接收机与时间复用两种BBM92实现，对二者共用的无源偏振分析器进行表征，并搭建时间复用接收机实验，使用CW泵浦的SPDC偏振纠缠光子对源和阈值探测器进行测量。

Result: 完成了无源偏振分析器的表征，并获得时间复用接收机的实验数据；在后处理阶段发现难以满足现有安全性证明的要求。

Conclusion: 理论安全假设与实际实现及可证明安全密钥提取之间存在明显差距，实际BBM92实现需谨慎对待安全证明的适用条件。

Abstract: The protocol introduced by C. H. Bennett, G. Brassard and N. D. Mermin in 1992 (BBM92) is one of the most important and widely adopted quantum key distribution (QKD) protocols. As with any other QKD protocol, its practical security depends on satisfying the assumptions underlying its theoretical security proofs. Here, we examine two common implementations of BBM92: a conventional passive receiver and a time-multiplexed configuration that halves the number of detectors. We characterise the passive polarisation analysers common to both architectures and present experimental results obtained with the time-multiplexed receiver, employing a continuous-wave (CW) pumped spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) source of polarisation-entangled photon pairs and threshold detectors. We investigate the challenges encountered during post-processing when attempting to satisfy the requirements of existing security proofs and discuss the gap between theoretical security assumptions and the extraction of a provably secure cryptographic key.

</details>


### [91] [Sharp rank thresholds and cubic support in PPT extensions of completely symmetric states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24500)
*Qian Lilong,Teng Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文给出完全对称态在PPT延拓层级下所需最小秩的精确公式（3d+N-3），并在相等情形将阶层的精确性归结为三元平面三次曲线上的奇点条件（除收缩二次曲线零轨迹外均为分裂实结点），从而对任意维度d给出首个精确层级/可行性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPT延拓层级是判定纠缠的重要工具，但"给定有限正层级下纠缠态的最小秩"以及"层级何时精确"此前缺乏与维度d无关的精确刻画。作者希望通过对称态的两/三体边缘约化到三元平面三次曲线上，获得可计算、可判定的精确阈值。

Method: 限制在d≥3方的完全对称态、实际局部秩N≥3上，考察通过任意给定有限正PPT延拓层级的纠缠态最小秩；利用两体与三体边缘共享同一三元平面三次曲线做约化；在光滑三次曲线上把沿指定路径（指向拐切线的平方）的阈值化为椭圆除法，并用反射不变路径得到四次公式与首项渐近常数；对rank-nine三qutrit有理态做显式构造检验。

Result: 纠缠态通过任意有限正层级的最小秩为3d+N-3，纠缠本身的最小秩为3d+N-5并完成等号分类；在等号处两、三体边缘复原同一条三次曲线，层级在有限阶精确当且仅当该三次曲线上位于收缩二次曲线零轨迹之外的所有奇点都是分裂实结点；对任意d给出首个精确层级或精确可行性判据；光滑三次曲线上沿指定路径的阈值化为椭圆除法且Gram矩阵（rank two）唯一，反射不变路径给出四次公式及渐近常数；给出一个显式有理rank-nine三qutrit态通过第一层、失败第二层。

Conclusion: 对称态的PPT延拓层级在其最小秩处由一条三元平面三次曲线完全支配，层级精确性与曲线奇点类型（分裂实结点）等价，阈值的计算可归约为椭圆除法；这提供了与维度无关的精确判据，并可由rank-nine三qutrit态例示层级间的真实分离（一层通过、二层失败）。

Abstract: For completely symmetric states on $d\ge3$ parties of actual local rank $N\ge3$, the least rank of an entangled state passing any prescribed finite positive level of the PPT extension hierarchy is $3d+N-3$. At equality, the two- and three-party marginals recover a ternary plane cubic shared by every extension. On this cubic the hierarchy becomes exact at a finite level precisely when every singularity outside the zero locus of the contraction quadratic is a split real node. We give the first exact level or an exact feasibility test for it, independent of $d$. On a smooth cubic the thresholds along the specified paths to the square of a flex tangent reduce to elliptic division and have unique rank-two Gram matrices. On reflection-invariant paths we obtain a quartic formula and the leading asymptotic constant. The least rank for entanglement itself is $3d+N-5$, and we classify equality. An explicit rational rank-nine three-qutrit state on a smooth cubic passes the first extension level and fails the second.

</details>

Result: 得到了入射态与出射态振幅和Born型概率之间的显式关系式；明确了这些关系在动力学群作用下的变换规律，从而建立不同时空框架间的观测关联；并发现某些常规守恒律的修订形式可能提供相对论纠缠形成的一般选择定则。

Conclusion: 研究表明，创建-湮灭过程中的自旋、螺旋度与极化态纠缠可在统一相对论框架内被系统表述；修订版的守恒律可充当相对论纠缠形成的普遍选择定则，为相对论量子纠缠的进一步研究提供形式化基础。

Abstract: It is suggested that a spontaneous formation of quantum mechanical entanglements does actually occur between spin, helicity and polarization states involved in creation-annihilation processes. A framework built up earlier that presumably affords a unified relativistic quantum mechanical description of free particle-antiparticle pairs of any spin is used to propose a comprehensive formulation of Wu-Shaknov entanglements. Explicit relationships between amplitudes and Born-like probabilities associated to incoming and outgoing states are obtained thereafter on the basis of certain theoretical assumptions. The behaviours of these relationships under the action of a dynamical group which is taken to supply observational correlations for different frames in spacetime are then specified. It appears that a modified version of some of the conventional conservation laws may afford a set of general selection rules for the formation of relativistic entanglements.

</details>


### [93] [On a local hidden-variable model for postselected EPR correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24534)
*Daniele Cenni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用基于sigmoid阈值机制的局部模型，在存在后选择（破坏公平采样假设）的条件下数值复现非最大纠缠两量子比特态的类量子关联，并给出CH–Eberhard判据与记忆型阈值扩展的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一个局部、依赖于测量设置的检测阈值模型能否在后选择样本中产生CHSH型违背，并厘清其与CHSH不等式、Mermin 82.8%效率界及无漏洞Bell实验之间的关系。

Method: 对|Phi_r>与|Psi_r>态在线性偏振测量下建模：以sigmoid阈值机制描述检测事件，检测与否依赖于测量设置，从而违背公平采样假设；在多种纠缠参数范围上做数值模拟，计算后选择检测样本中的CHSH量，并用单侧单探测器的CH–Eberhard参数J检验局域性；进一步引入实现单侧记忆的局部自适应阈值机制，与固定阈值模型做配对比较。

Result: 在被测配置中，模型仅通过依赖于设置的检测机制在后选择样本中产生CHSH型违背，处于CHSH不等式与Mermin 82.8%效率界的假设之外；CH–Eberhard分析在整个纠缠参数范围内均给出J<0，符合严格局域模型要求。自适应阈值记忆规则使J相对固定阈值模型产生系统性、可重复的偏移，但CH–Eberhard分析在测试条件下定性不变。

Conclusion: 局部的、依赖于设置的后选择机制能够系统性地塑造Bell检验统计量；该模型不构成对无漏洞Bell实验的挑战，因其利用的正是这些实验已封闭的检测漏洞；结论限于所测试的具体配置与阈值规则。

Abstract: A theoretical and numerical analysis of a Bell-type experiment is presented, where linear polarization measurements on nonmaximally entangled two-qubit states are modeled through a sigmoid-based threshold mechanism that violates the fair-sampling assumption required by the CHSH inequality. Numerical simulations characterize how closely this local construction can reproduce quantum-like correlations in the postselected detected sample, for both the |Phi_r> and |Psi_r> states over a range of entanglement parameters. For the tested configurations, the model produces CHSH-type violations only in the postselected detected sample through a setting-dependent detection mechanism, operating outside the assumptions of both the CHSH inequality and Mermin's 82.8% efficiency bound. The model is not in conflict with loophole-free Bell experiments, which specifically close the detection loophole exploited here. The CH--Eberhard analysis in the one-detector-per-side configuration yields J < 0 throughout the full range of entanglement parameters, as required for a strictly local model. The model is further extended with a local adaptive-threshold mechanism implementing one-sided memory. A paired comparison shows that this specific memory rule produces a systematic and reproducible shift in the CH--Eberhard parameter J relative to the fixed-threshold model, while leaving the CH--Eberhard analysis qualitatively unchanged under the tested conditions. The results characterize how a local, setting-dependent postselection mechanism shapes Bell-test statistics across a range of entanglement parameters, within the specific configurations and threshold rules tested.

</details>


### [94] [No-Go Theorems for Ordered Universal Entanglement Purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24603)
*C. L. Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究"有序通用纠缠纯化"：在仅知输入纠缠对保真度排序、状态本身刻画不完全的情形下，证明对任意有限n≥2，任何不降低最高输入保真度的可分离成功映射，一旦成功必精确保持该保真度，从而排除非平凡通用LOCC纯化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子系统（如量子中继网络）中，有限内存、概率性纠缠产生与不等存储时间会留下有限个非全同纠缠对；这些态可能刻画不完全，但制备与存储记录可给出保真度排序。此前仅对受限LOCC类给出no-go结果，一般LOCC下是否存在非平凡通用纯化仍悬而未决。

Method: 将LOCC放宽到可分离操作（separable operations），对每个固定有限n≥2，令独立制备的双量子比特对在固定保真度阈值F*以上（0≤F*<1）的整个态族上取值，分析"从不降低最高输入保真度"的可分离成功映射，并证明该映射在成功时必须精确保持最高保真度。证明不施加成功概率正下界。

Result: 证明了上述no-go定理：任何可分离成功映射，若在所有允许的有序输入上从不降低最高输入保真度，则每次成功时都精确保持该最高保真度，因而无法对任何联合输入严格提升它；该结论对任意高的输入保真度成立，且不依赖成功概率下界。

Conclusion: 有序通用纠缠纯化在可分离操作（因而在一般LOCC）下不存在非平凡方案，彻底否定了一般LOCC下通用有序纯化的可能性。

Abstract: Entanglement purification is a fundamental tool for obtaining high-fidelity entangled states used in various quantum technologies. It aims to increase the fidelity of noisy shared entangled states with a fixed maximally entangled state through LOCC. In distributed quantum systems, such as networks employing quantum repeaters, limited memory, probabilistic entanglement generation, and unequal storage times can leave a finite collection of nonidentical pairs. Their states may remain incompletely characterized even when calibrated preparation and storage records establish their fidelity ordering. Recent work has investigated whether fidelity ordering permits nontrivial universal purification. A nontrivial universal $n$-to-1 protocol must, conditioned on success, never lower the highest input fidelity and strictly increase it for at least one allowed joint input. Although no-go results have been established for a restricted class of LOCC protocols, the corresponding question for general LOCC has remained unresolved. Here, we study ordered universal purification under separable operations. Specifically, for every fixed finite $n\ge2$, we prove that when independently prepared two-qubit pairs range over the full family of states above a fixed fidelity threshold $0\le F_*<1$, any separable success map that never lowers the highest input fidelity on the allowed ordered inputs must preserve that fidelity exactly whenever it succeeds. This excludes nontrivial universal LOCC purification even for arbitrarily high input fidelities, without imposing a positive lower bound on success probability. ...

</details>


### [95] [Measurement-order dependence bounds uncertainty, contextuality, steering, and Bell nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24604)
*Shengjun Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入“次序得分”（总变差距离 T、Jensen-Shannon 散度 J、平方 Hellinger 距离）量化同一输入下两种相反次序量子测量的联合分布差异，证明其严格刻画交换性，并将其与 Maassen-Uffink 熵界、非定域性/上下文性/Bell 不等式违背定量联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个量子测量以相反次序作用于同一输入会产生两个不同的联合分布，但缺乏一个统一的、可操作的度量来刻画“测量次序效应”的大小，且该效应对量子关联强度（纠缠、违背、互无偏性）的意义未被系统刻画。

Method: 以 Lüders 仪器为对象定义三种次序得分（T、J、平方 Hellinger）；证明其为零当且仅当测量交换，并对两个二值锐观测给出 T=‖[A,B]‖/4；对投射测量由 Maassen-Uffink 界 q 推出 T≥1−2^{-q} 与 J 的正下界；将场景中全部可观测量顺序测量得到联合分布，用次序得分界定非上下文性、导向与 Bell 不等式违背；对相关型 Bell 不等式证明平方 Bell 算子等于对称（Jordan）部分减去局域对易子张量积，并在一方两个（Clifford 实现下至多三个）设置时证明双线性界，依据 Hilbert-Schmidt 型 Grothendieck 不等式；分别分析三二值测量下 Bell 原始不等式的次序效应表达式，以及 qutrit 上秩一 KCBS 五边形的界。

Result: 某些基对比互无偏基（MUB）具有更大的平方 Hellinger（奇数维下 J 也更大）得分，尽管其最小熵和更小；Maassen-Uffink 界 q>0 蕴含 T≥1−2^{-q} 及 J 的正下界；相关型 Bell 不等式违背可由两方次序得分的双线性式上界（一方两设置时为定理，一般情形为猜想）；Bell 原始不等式的最大纠缠态违背等于最大混态三次测量记录中的次序效应减去四倍交替概率；KCBS 五边形违背不超过两条交叉对角线次序得分之积的最大值，且在最大违背处取等号。

Conclusion: 测量次序差异构成一个统一、可计算且具物理意义的资源量：它既是交换性的精确判据，又定量控制量子关联违背强度，并为 Bell 不等式违背提供基于局域对易子与 Grothendieck 不等式的结构解释。

Abstract: Two quantum measurements applied to the same input in opposite orders give two joint distributions of the same outcomes. For Lüders instruments we measure how far apart these order records can be, over all inputs, by their total-variation distance $\mathcal{T}$, their Jensen-Shannon divergence $\mathcal{J}$ (in bits), and their squared Hellinger distance. These order scores vanish exactly when the measurements commute, and $\mathcal{T}=\|[A,B]\|/4$ for two binary sharp observables. Some pairs of bases exceed mutually unbiased bases in squared Hellinger score, and in odd dimensions in $\mathcal{J}$, although their minimal entropy sum is smaller. For projective measurements, a Maassen-Uffink bound $q>0$ implies $\mathcal{T}\ge1-2^{-q}$ and a positive lower bound on $\mathcal{J}$. Measuring all observables of a scenario in sequence defines a joint distribution, so violations of noncontextuality, steering, and Bell inequalities are bounded by distances between order records. For correlation Bell inequalities with binary sharp observables, the squared Bell operator is a symmetrized (Jordan) part minus tensor products of local commutators. If one party has two settings, the violation is bounded bilinearly in the two parties' order scores; we conjecture this bound in general. On Clifford realizations it would follow from a Hilbert-Schmidt variant of Grothendieck's inequality, which we prove when one party has at most three settings. For three binary sharp measurements, the violation of Bell's original inequality by a maximally entangled state equals an order effect in three-measurement records of the maximally mixed state minus four times an alternation probability. For the rank-one Klyachko-Can-Binicioğlu-Shumovsky pentagon on a qutrit, the violation is at most the largest product of the order scores of two crossing diagonals, with equality at the maximal violation.

</details>


### [96] [Number-correlated photon entanglement with a quantum memory interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24605)
*Imogen Forbes,Vincent Boyer,Louis Edwards-Pratt,Mahirul Khan,Sougato Bose,Thomas M Hird*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将量子存储器嵌入马赫-曾德尔干涉仪，借助非共振阶梯型原子相互作用，无需传统光学非线性即可实现光子数基下的确定性变换 Σa_n|n⟩→Σa_n|n⟩|n⟩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单光子水平上的强光子-光子相互作用是量子光学技术的核心需求，但难以实现。

Method: 在马赫-曾德尔干涉仪中嵌入量子存储器；利用非共振阶梯型原子相互作用，通过驱动控制场将存储—取回过程把单光子激发映射为跨不同光学模式的光子对；干涉结构使该过程确定性给出光子数关联变换。

Result: 实现确定性的 Σa_n|n⟩→Σa_n|n⟩|n⟩ 变换；测量一个模式即可实现光子非破坏探测、态制备与频率转换等应用，并可产生高斯与非高斯纠缠态。

Conclusion: 量子存储器+干涉架构构成实现测量诱导光子-光子相互作用与纠缠态生成的通用平台。

Abstract: Strong photon-photon interactions are central to quantum optical technologies yet remain challenging to realise at the single-photon level. Here, we show how embedding a quantum memory within a Mach-Zehnder interferometer enables a coherent transformation that converts single-mode photon states into number-correlated entangled states. Using an off-resonant ladder-type atomic interaction, storage and retrieval within a quantum memory maps a single-photon excitation onto a pair of photons across distinct optical modes via a driving control field. Embedding this within an interferometer deterministically produces an effective transformation in the photon number basis: $\sum_n a_n |n\rangle \to \sum_n a_n |n\rangle |n\rangle$ without relying on conventional optical nonlinearities. Measurement of one mode enables photonic non-demolition detection, state preparation, and frequency conversion as applications of the same underlying transformation. These results establish quantum memories embedded within an interferometric architecture as a platform for measurement-induced photon-photon interactions and the generation of Gaussian and non-Gaussian entangled states.

</details>


### [97] [Instanton tunnelling as a verifiable benchmark for digital quantum simulation: circuit structure, classical simulability and hardware reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24624)
*Gerhard Hellstern,Marc Maussner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 以四重双势阱中的瞬子介导隧穿为可精确校验的基准，在数字量子模拟框架下研究单激发（unary）编码格点模型的隧穿动力学，并给出门数标度与超导硬件实测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一个每一步都能与精确解对照的基准问题来评估数字量子模拟的保真度；瞬子隧穿指数小，对相干性和截断误差极为敏感，是理想的可验证靶标。

Method: 瞬子由经典分析给出，硬件验证格点模型动力学。N 个格点映射到 N 个量子比特（单激发编码），以经典可算的单激发动力学为参考。对每个键上相邻等角 XX/YY 对的二阶 Trotter 电路，证明任意割的 Schmidt 秩在每个门边界≤4、每对门之后≤2，且与 N 无关；据此做 MPS 模拟并与精确能级劈裂比对。采用键逐项（kinetic）与 kinetic/potential 两种分裂比较 Trotter 步长标度；在步长较粗但有独立界的情形下，用两台超导处理器在 N=8 测量完整转移。

Result: MPS 模拟复现精确劈裂至 N=48。键逐项公式的可容许 Trotter 步长约按 N^-2 缩小，一个隧穿周期约需 N^3.3 个两比特门；kinetic/potential 分裂在 N=128 内步长仍近 0.065，说明该标度来源于逐键分裂动能项。硬件上 N=8 测到完整转移，格点劈裂复原到几个百分点，并重现其随势垒高度的依赖（此分辨率下劈裂由格点而非瞬子决定）。9 次重复：相隔数分钟的运行在散粒噪声内一致，相隔数小时至数周者差异达 6.1%（同一固定量子比特集亦如此）；以 session 为抽样单位时，两处理器在阻尼上有差异，而劈裂差异未被确立。按实测单激发布居归一化可将可用深度扩展约 4 倍，但不改善劈裂；无需对角化的高斯初态与基于本征矢的初态劈裂相差在 1.6 个百分点内。

Conclusion: 该基准表明单激发编码下的键逐项 Trotter 电路具有与 N 无关的低 Schmidt 秩，可用经典 MPS 精确校验；但逐键分裂动能的步长标度（门数 ~N^3.3）是其主要资源瓶颈，改用 kinetic/potential 分裂可显著缓解。硬件层面，格点劈裂可由当前超导处理器以几个百分点精度复原，残余误差主要来自随时间的漂移与阻尼差异而非可复现的系统偏差。

Abstract: We use instanton-mediated tunnelling in the quartic double well as a benchmark for digital quantum simulation that is checkable against an exact answer at every step. The instanton enters through the classical analysis; the hardware tests the dynamics of the lattice model. A unary (single-excitation) encoding maps N lattice sites to N qubits, with the single-excitation dynamics as a reference that remains classically cheap at any N. For a second-order Trotter circuit with adjacent, equal-angle XX/YY pairs on each bond, we prove that the Schmidt rank across any cut is at most four at every gate boundary and at most two after each pair, independently of N. Matrix-product-state simulations of this gate list reproduce the exact splitting up to N = 48. For this bond-wise formula the admissible Trotter step shrinks roughly as N^-2, so one tunnelling period costs about N^3.3 two-qubit gates; a kinetic/potential splitting keeps the step near 0.065 up to N=128, so this scaling belongs to splitting the kinetic term bond by bond. At a coarser, separately bounded step we measure the full transfer at N=8 on two superconducting processors, recovering the lattice splitting to a few percent and its dependence on the barrier height, which at this resolution is set by the lattice rather than by the instanton. Over 9 repetitions, runs taken minutes apart agree to shot noise, while runs hours to weeks apart differ by up to 6.1%, also on a fixed set of qubits; with sessions as the sampling unit, the processors differ in the damping, while a difference in the splitting is not established. Normalising by the measured single-excitation population extends the usable depth about 4-fold but does not improve the splitting. A Gaussian start state, which needs no diagonalisation, reproduces the splitting of the eigenvector-based start to within 1.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [98] [On Reaction Depth and Fast Fault-Tolerant Boolean Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24687)
*Christopher Kang,Alexander Cowtan,Adam Bene Watts,Guillaume Dauphinais,Benjamin Ide*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文形式化了量子程序"反应深度"（reaction depth）概念，并证明在LDPC码友好的齐次PPM+Toffoli态门集下，任意布尔算术电路可用常数量子深度与常数反应深度实现（代价是指数空间与脏比特），并给出高效的波纹进位加法器与教科书乘法器实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子程序运行时间不仅取决于门速度，还取决于程序需等待经典协处理器计算自适应操作的次数；近期降低该开销的思路是压缩"反应深度"，但该概念此前缺乏严格定义，且其与magic depth等概念的关系存在混淆之处，需要厘清。

Method: 首先形式化反应深度，区分"电路的反应深度"与"电路单次运行的反应深度"，并分析Clifford化+magic态注入编译后magic深度可高于反应深度等微妙之处。随后聚焦相干布尔逻辑这一昂贵组件，采用齐次Z型与X型Pauli积测量（PPM）加Toffoli态供给的门集；证明任意布尔算术电路可常数量子深度、常数反应深度实现；构造两个仅需单个Toffoli态与齐次PPM的高效AutoAND电路；并给出量子波纹进位加法器与量子schoolbook乘法器实现，其uncomputation仅需少量异质PPM。

Result: 得到常数量子深度与常数反应深度的布尔算术电路实现（代价为指数空间及部分"脏"量子比特）；构造出两个高效AutoAND电路；波纹进位加法器与schoolbook乘法器在受限门集下仍与当前最优实现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 反应深度是可严格定义且有实际意义的复杂度度量，但需区分电路与运行层面；在LDPC码适配的齐次PPM门集下，常数深度算术在原理上可行，且在加法器、乘法器等具体构件上可达到实用竞争力。

Abstract: Reducing runtimes of quantum applications is critical for practical viability. On quantum computers, the runtime can depend both on the speed of quantum operations and on how often the program must wait for the classical coprocessor to determine adaptive operations. Some recent approaches to reducing this overhead have focused on minimizing the "reaction depth" of a computation, informally defined as the number of times the classical coprocessor must be consulted while running a quantum program.
  In this work, we formalize the notion of reaction depth. We find several subtleties in this theory. For example, we distinguish the reaction depth of a circuit from that of a circuit run, and show that the magic depth of a unitary can be higher than the reaction depth once compiled into Clifford computation and magic state injection.
  We then focus on the specific case of coherent Boolean logic, a common and expensive component in quantum algorithms. We use a natural gateset for fast operations with Low-Density Parity Check (LDPC) codes, which consists of homogeneous $Z$- and $X$-type Pauli Product Measurements (PPMs), alongside a supply of Toffoli states. We prove that, using this gateset, any Boolean arithmetic circuit can be implemented on a quantum computer with constant quantum depth and constant reaction depth, at the cost of exponential space and leaving some qubits "dirty". Using the same gateset we find two efficient AutoAND circuits that require only a Toffoli state and homogeneous PPMs. We then give efficient implementations of the quantum ripple-carry adder and quantum schoolbook multiplier, which require a low number of heterogeneous PPMs for uncomputation. We find implementations competitive with the state-of-the-art despite the restricted gateset.

</details>


### [99] [Coherent-Response and Thermal-Fluctuation Information in Cavity-Magnon Magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24701)
*G. Zhao,X. -F. Qian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将腔-磁振子体系中微波正交分量涨落的方差作为磁场信息的独立来源，推导高斯似然并把Fisher信息精确分解为相干位移项与热涨落项，比较两者最优工作条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔-磁振子磁传感通常只利用相干响应，把场依赖的涨落当作测量噪声；作者希望表明方差本身携带可用的磁场信息。

Method: 对定标正交分量做重复相位敏感测量，推导高斯似然与Fisher信息，得到位移项（依赖正交均值对磁场的导数）与热涨落项（依赖方差对磁场的导数）；在近共振近似下解析求极值，并数值/解析映射两项相对贡献随驱动频率、驱动强度与温度的变化。

Result: 振幅正交的归一化相干响应因子在协同度 C=2（g=√(2κ_aκ_m)）及归一化失谐 δ_a=δ_m=±1 处达全局最大；近共振近似下约化方差信息项在腔-磁振子频率匹配 Δ_a=Δ_m 处全局最大，并在大协同度下趋于有限极限。

Conclusion: 强相干驱动有利于基于位移的传感；有限温度下弱驱动时方差贡献可占主导，且即使相干位移贡献消失仍保持有限。由此建立按测量方式区分的腔-磁振子磁强计框架，并给出利用正交均值、方差或两者的工作区间。

Abstract: Magnetic sensing with cavity--magnon systems typically relies on the coherent response, while field-dependent fluctuations are treated as measurement noise. Here, we show that the variance of a measured microwave quadrature provides a distinct source of magnetic-field information. For repeated phase-sensitive measurements of a calibrated quadrature, we derive the Gaussian likelihood and show that its Fisher information separates exactly into two terms. The coherent-displacement term depends on the field derivative of the quadrature mean, and the thermal-fluctuation term depends on the field derivative of its variance. Both terms are accessible from the same measurement record, but their optimal operating conditions differ. For the amplitude quadrature, the dimensionless coherent response factor reaches its global maximum at cooperativity $C=2$, corresponding to a coupling strength $g=\sqrt{2κ_aκ_m}$, and at linewidth-normalized detunings $δ_a=δ_m=\pm 1$. In the near-resonance approximation, the reduced variance-information term is globally maximized at cavity--magnon frequency matching, $Δ_a=Δ_m$, and approaches a finite limit at large cooperativity. We map the relative contributions of the two terms across drive frequencies, drive strengths, and temperatures. Strong coherent driving favors displacement-based sensing, whereas at finite temperature the variance contribution can dominate under weak driving and remain finite even when the coherent-displacement contribution vanishes. These results establish a measurement-specific framework for cavity--magnon magnetometry and identify operating regimes for exploiting the quadrature mean, its variance, or both.

</details>


### [100] [Squeezed Cooling and Lasing with Cold Atoms in Cavity-QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24743)
*Rodrigo Grande de Diego,Carlos Sánchez Muñoz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出无需非线性晶体与外注入压缩光的方案：利用铷原子系综与压缩腔模的腔辅助拉曼跃迁，先以"压缩冷却"把腔内场压缩至9 dB、输出压缩达18 dB，再以非相干泵浦驱动激光跃迁进入压缩腔模，实现相干时间长、宽兆赫兹带内低于真空噪声的"压缩激光"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩激光兼具激光的亮度/相干性与单正交分量低于真空噪声的特性，但现有实现依赖非线性晶体或注入压缩光，实验复杂、压缩度受限（参量振荡器3 dB极限）。作者希望寻找纯原子系统、无需外部压缩源的替代光学方案。

Method: 基于两套铷原子系综与同一压缩腔模的腔辅助拉曼跃迁工程化相互作用：第一系综将腔模冷却为压缩真空（称"压缩冷却"，并可通过调谐转为输出模式），第二系综经非相干泵浦实现向压缩腔模的激光跃迁；结合阈值、超辐射与线宽的理论/数值分析刻画系统。

Result: 腔内场压缩最高约9 dB，突破光参量振荡器的3 dB限制；调谐至输出时输出压缩达18 dB。压缩激光输出为明亮光束、相干时间长，低于真空噪声的带宽达兆赫兹量级，避开载频处尖锐相位扩散过量噪声峰，并鲁棒于弛豫振荡；给出了激光阈值、超辐射发射与线宽窄化的特征与可实验实现的参数区间。

Conclusion: 该方案以原子拉曼增益—损耗工程化方式，在不使用非线性晶体和注入压缩光的条件下实现压缩激光，且压缩度显著超越参量振荡器极限，为可实验实现的压缩激光器提供清晰路径。

Abstract: A squeezed laser undergoes a lasing transition into a squeezed cavity mode, where stimulated emission generates a bright coherent field with a laser-like coherence time while maintaining one field quadrature below the vacuum noise level. Here we propose an optical implementation that needs neither a nonlinear crystal nor injected squeezed light, based on engineered interactions between ensembles of rubidium atoms and a squeezed cavity mode via cavity-assisted Raman transitions. A first ensemble cools the cavity into a squeezed vacuum, a mechanism we term squeezed cooling, yielding up to 9 dB in the intracavity field, surpassing the 3 dB limit of optical parametric oscillators. Tuned for output instead, it reaches up to 18 dB of output squeezing. Incoherently pumping a second ensemble drives a lasing transition into the squeezed mode, emitting a bright beam with long coherence time and noise below vacuum spanning a megahertz-wide band, away from the sharp phase-diffusion peak of excess noise at the carrier frequency, and robust to relaxation oscillations. We characterize lasing thresholds, superradiant emission, and linewidth narrowing, and identify the experimentally accessible regime where the system exhibits the defining features of a squeezed laser.

</details>


### [101] [Environment-assisted imaginary time evolution in feedback-based quantum algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24783)
*J. C. Bellizotti Souza,M. C. de Oliveira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出CITE-FALQON：以开放量子系统框架将虚时演化（ITE）作为环境诱导的量子操作，与FALQON反馈律结合，避免经典参数优化与非幺正ITE近似，在ANNNI链与MaxCut上可从任意初态制备基态且平均所需层数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FALQON无需经典参数优化即可制备基态，但电路深、易因贫瘠高原与对称性保护而停滞在非基态。引入ITE层可增强收敛，但闭系中非幺正ITE不可实现，需近似；因此用开放量子系统表述把ITE纳入FALQON更合适。

Method: 将ITE描述为由环境诱导的量子操作（开放系统表述），并与FALQON反馈律结合，构成CITE-FALQON算法；在受挫Ising链（ANNNI）与MaxCut问题上进行数值模拟，比较从任意初态及固定初态下的收敛性与所需层数。

Result: CITE-FALQON可从任意初态制备基态，包括FALQON失效的情形；在固定初态下，对两个问题平均所需层数更少。其优势强烈依赖ANNNI链的系统相以及MaxCut问题的图权重。

Conclusion: 以开放量子系统表述引入ITE可有效缓解FALQON的停滞问题，实现无需经典优化的基态制备；但性能提升与问题结构（相位、图权重）密切相关，并非普遍一致。

Abstract: The Feedback-based Algorithm for Quantum Optimization (FALQON) prepares ground states without classical parameter optimization, but requires deep circuits and can stagnate away from the ground state as a consequence of barren plateaus and symmetry protection. In order to avoid stagnation, imaginary time evolution (ITE) layers can be incorporated into FALQON layers, enhancing FALQON convergence. However, applying a non-unitary ITE layer in a closed quantum system is not possible, and approximations must be made to use ITE on real hardware; using an open quantum system formulation to incorporate ITE into FALQON represents a better approach. We describe ITE as a quantum operation induced by an environment, and combine it with the FALQON feedback law. In simulations of a frustrated Ising chain (ANNNI) and of MaxCut, the resulting algorithm prepares the ground state starting from any initial state, where FALQON can fail, and needs fewer layers on average when the initial state is fixed for the two investigated problems. The CITE-FALQON advantage strongly depends on the system phase for the ANNNI chain, and on the graph weights for the MaxCut problem.

</details>


### [102] [Soft Mixture of Quantum Experts for Hybrid Quantum-Classical Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24798)
*Valeria Repetto,Romina D'Aurizio,Piero Farinelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出Soft-MoQE——用经典门控网络对固定量子特征映射做输入相关加权组合，避免变分量子训练，性能媲美可训练量子神经网络且量子训练成本降低数个量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期QML架构依赖可训练参数化量子线路，受硬件资源受限、噪声与贫瘠高原等优化困难制约。

Method: 构建异质池的非可训练量子线路，每条线路定义一种不同嵌入；由经典门控网络学习对每个数据点的专家权重，实现软混合（Soft Mixture of Quantum Experts）。

Result: 在三个PCA降维任务（MNIST奇偶、MNIST十分类、FashionMNIST）上，优于最佳单专家、专家均匀平均与线性基线，性能与可训练量子神经网络相当；类别条件专家使用分析显示每个输入仅组合少数专家且选择依赖类别。

Conclusion: 固定量子线路间的可学习路由可作为兼顾可训练性的混合QML设计原则。

Abstract: Quantum Machine Learning (QML) has attracted increasing interest as a possible route to construct feature representations that are difficult to reproduce with classical models. However, near-term QML architectures based on trainable parametrized quantum circuits remain affected by limited hardware resources, noise, and severe optimization difficulties such as barren plateaus. In this work we propose Soft-MoQE, a Soft Mixture of Quantum Experts architecture that replaces variational quantum training with the input-dependent selection and combination of fixed quantum feature maps. The model is composed of a heterogeneous pool of non-trainable quantum circuits, each defining a distinct embedding, and a classical gating network that learns how to weight these embeddings for each data point. We benchmark Soft-MoQE on three PCA-reduced tasks: MNIST parity, MNIST 10-class, and FashionMNIST. Soft-MoQE outperforms the single best expert, the uniform average of all experts and a linear baseline, achieving task-dependent performance comparable to that of a trainable quantum neural network, without optimizing any quantum parameter and with a quantum training cost that is lower by orders of magnitude. The analysis of the class- conditioned expert usage shows that the learned routing combines a few experts for each input, with a selection that depends on the class. These results support the learned routing among fixed quantum circuits as a trainability-aware design principle for hybrid QML.

</details>


### [103] [Shedding Light on Quantum Non-Classicality: Witnessing Wigner Negativity with Free Electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24834)
*Sunjun Hwang,Leong Chuan Kwek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于相位分辨量子光子诱导近场电子显微镜(QPINEM)的噪声鲁棒框架，无需完整光学层析即可见证光学Wigner负性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认证Wigner负性等量子非经典性通常依赖完整的光学态层析，实验代价高且对噪声敏感，亟需一种可绕过完整重构、且能在真实实验条件下可靠出具的见证方案。

Method: 将电子相干性直接映射到耦合受限相空间区域内对称排序的光学特征函数；依据Bochner定理，由记录的电子谱构造测量后见证矩阵，以负本征值判定非经典性；并引入噪声感知的筛选流程，将"发现"与"统计验证"解耦以保证可靠性。

Result: 在真空-Fock叠加态、奇猫态与GKP态等多种光学制备上进行了基准测试，方案均能给出具有统计界（statistically bounded）的量子特征探测结果。

Conclusion: 该协议为利用自由电子探测光学量子特性提供了一条规避完整层析、噪声鲁棒且统计可界定的路径。

Abstract: Certifying quantum non-classicality like Wigner negativity typically demands full optical state tomography. This work introduces a noise-resilient framework for witnessing optical Wigner negativity using phase-resolved quantum photon-induced near-field electron microscopy (QPINEM). By mapping electron coherences directly to symmetrically ordered optical characteristic functions within a coupling-limited phase-space region, the protocol bypasses full optical reconstruction. A post-measurement witness matrix is constructed via Bochner's theorem from recorded electron spectra, where negative eigenvalues certify non-classicality. To ensure reliability under realistic experimental conditions, a noise-aware selection procedure decouples discovery from statistical validation. Benchmarked across various optical preparations-including vacuum-Fock superpositions, odd cat states, and GKP states-the protocol provides a statistically bounded pathway for probing quantum features with free electrons.

</details>


### [104] [The limits of magic-state cultivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24852)
*Llorenç Balada Gaggioli,Jakub Mareček*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文指出Pauli代数无法刻画非Clifford门下的容错，提出用"非Clifford门上的稳定子码"这一统一框架来精确判定magic state制备电路中哪些故障有害，并将其应用于cultivation的S门代理分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错分析传统上依赖Pauli代数，但该代数在非Clifford门处失效。像magic-state cultivation这样用物理T门检验编码T态的电路，因而长期缺乏"哪些故障有害"的精确理论，设计只能依赖S门等代理，可能高估容错能力。

Method: 建立基于"非Clifford门上单个稳定子码"的故障理论：对任意用非Clifford门加后选择制备magic state的电路，由该码判定故障是否有害；对理想编码态的单次检验，故障距离退化为该码上的Pauli代数；以此严格分析S门代理与现有color/surface code检验，并构造保持全距离的最近邻检验。

Result: 给出cultivation的T-count下界与精确检验；证明S门代理只会高估此类检验的故障距离；现有color code与surface code检验至多容忍其编码所允许故障数的一半；所构造的最近邻检验保持完整故障距离，并在码距9以内得到认证。

Conclusion: 非Clifford门上的稳定子码为magic state制备电路提供了精确的故障理论，统一了cultivation与distillation，并修正了以S门作代理的设计实践；该框架还能指导设计既保持全距离又满足近邻约束的检验电路。

Abstract: Fault tolerance is understood through Pauli algebra, which fails at non-Clifford gates. Circuits such as magic-state cultivation, which checks an encoded T state with physical T gates, have therefore lacked an exact theory of which faults are harmful. We develop such a theory and show that, for any circuit that prepares a magic state with non-Clifford gates and postselection, one stabilizer code on the non-Clifford gates decides which faults are harmful. This ties cultivation to distillation, bounds its T-count from below and gives an exact test. For a single check on an ideally encoded state, the fault distance reduces to Pauli algebra on the code. As a use case, we analyze the S gates that have stood in for T gates in the design of cultivation. We show that this proxy can only overstate the fault distance of such a check, and that the checks in use, on color and surface codes, tolerate at most half as many faults as their code allows. The theory also yields nearest-neighbor checks that keep the full distance, certified up to code distance nine.

</details>


### [105] [Hurst Exponent Estimation with Quantum Wavelets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24882)
*Brani Vidakovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究在量子框架下对单一振幅编码分形布朗运动路径重复测量时Hurst指数$H$的可估计精度，通过改进$\epsilon$-抽取量子非抽取小波变换（去除循环滤波端点跳变、推导估计量抽样分布、在等查询预算下比较读出方案）给出理论保证与实证精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题：当每次测量都询问同一条实现路径时，$H$能被多精确地估计？现有$\epsilon$-抽取量子非抽取小波变换存在循环滤波端点跳变问题，且缺乏估计量的抽样分布理论与公平的读出方案比较。

Method: 对$\epsilon$-抽取量子非抽取小波变换做三方面扩展：(1) 引入单量子比特镜像延拓，消除循环滤波在折叠处的端点跳变，使跨越折叠的系数与内部系数同阶，适用于任意紧支撑滤波器与循环移位；(2) 由滤波器组的准酉晶格编译任意此类滤波器的变换，能级能量份额等于一次多重受控反射的成功概率（该反射既是Hadamard检验的受控酉，也是振幅估计的oracle）；(3) 对至少有两个消失矩的滤波器，证明对数能量对比的根$N$中心极限定理，与极限路径范数无关，使student化估计量在每层成功计数发散的确定性查询预算下渐近标准正态；对稀疏计数给出多项—对数正态似然（在直接测量读出模型下精确），并用模拟检验其覆盖率。

Result: 长度512的路径上，镜像使$H\ge0.5$时的RMSE降低3–10倍；在精确份额下非抽取db2估计量高出根Cramér–Rao界6–14%；Hadamard能级检验在任何预算下均不如直接测量前导量子比特；$H=0.9$、$2^{20}$次查询时，最大似然振幅估计RMSE为0.0347，直接测量为0.0451，精确份额为0.0295。

Conclusion: 镜像延拓显著提升估计精度，根$N$极限理论为student化估计量提供渐近推断基础；读出方案上直接测量前导量子比特优于Hadamard能级检验，而最大似然振幅估计在给定查询预算下最接近精确份额基准；非抽取db2估计量已较接近根Cramér–Rao界。

Abstract: How accurately can the Hurst exponent $H$ of fractional Brownian motion be estimated from repeated preparations of a single amplitude-encoded path, when every measurement interrogates the same realization? We extend the $ε$-decimated quantum nondecimated wavelet transform in three directions: we remove the endpoint jump of circular filtering, derive the sampling distribution of the estimator, and compare readouts at equal query budgets. A one-qubit mirror extension removes the jump and keeps every coefficient that straddles a fold of the same order as an interior coefficient, for any compactly supported filter and circular shift. The transform for any such filter is compiled from the paraunitary lattice of its filter bank, and the energy share of a level is the success probability of one multiply-controlled reflection, which is both the controlled unitary of a Hadamard test and the oracle of amplitude estimation. For filters with at least two vanishing moments we prove a root-$N$ central limit theorem for the log-energy contrasts, independent of the limiting path norm, so the studentized estimator is asymptotically standard normal for deterministic query budgets under which the success count at every level diverges. For sparse counts we give a working multinomial--lognormal likelihood, exact in its readout model for direct measurement, and check its coverage by simulation. For paths of length $512$, mirroring reduces RMSE by a factor of $3$--$10$ for $H\ge0.5$, and with exact shares the nondecimated db2 estimator is $6$--$14\%$ above the root Cramér--Rao bound. Hadamard level tests are less accurate than direct measurement of the leading qubits at every budget. At $H=0.9$ and $2^{20}$ queries, maximum-likelihood amplitude estimation attains RMSE $0.0347$, against $0.0451$ for direct measurement and $0.0295$ with exact shares.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum Simulation of First-Quantised Chemistry with a Sum-of-Squares Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24885)
*Aleksei V. Ivanov,Christoph Sünderhauf,Jon Kunjummen,Nick S. Blunt,Róbert Izsák*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于非凸平方和哈密顿量表示与谱隙放大的第一量子化相位估计算法，在低量子比特、低占据率区间优于第二量子化方法，最多节省16倍Toffoli门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子能量计算是量子计算的重要应用，但主流高效算法所需逻辑量子比特数巨大，扩展至数千比特工程难度高；且多数基准测试采用的活性空间轨道数与电子数相当，缺失描述真实化学所需的动态相关。真实化学即使在紧凑原子轨道基组下也需轨道数为电子数的数倍，处于低占据率区间，该区间理论上利于第一量子化（每电子所需量子比特随轨道数仅对数增长），但其在真实规模基组下是否具有实用优势尚不明确。

Method: 构建适用于一般轨道基组的第一量子化相位估计算法：采用哈密顿量的非凸平方和（sum-of-squares）表示，并结合谱隙放大（spectral-gap amplification）；与最先进的第二量子化算法在相同逻辑层面编译技术下进行对比。

Result: 对6个分子（4–24个电子，轨道数为电子数的5–14倍）进行测试：在仅数百个逻辑量子比特的低比特区间，第一量子化方法所需Toffoli门比第二量子化最多少16倍；其优势可维持至数百到约1400个量子比特；最小量子比特数比第二量子化低1.4–2倍。

Conclusion: 在低量子比特数与低占据率区间，所提第一量子化算法是轨道基组化学模拟的首选方法。

Abstract: Calculating molecular energies is expected to be an important application of quantum computers, but the most efficient algorithms to achieve this typically have significant space requirements. Scaling quantum processors to thousands of logical qubits is a daunting engineering challenge, so developing algorithms that run with reduced qubit requirements is of particular importance. Moreover, most algorithms are benchmarked on active spaces with about as many orbitals as electrons, missing dynamical correlation which is necessary to describe real chemistry. Capturing this correlation requires several times more orbitals than electrons, even in compact atomic-orbital basis sets. This low-filling regime favours first quantisation, where each electron is represented by a number of qubits that grows only logarithmically with the number of orbitals. Whether this approach translates into a practical advantage in orbital basis sets of realistic size, however, has been unclear. Here, we develop a first-quantised phase estimation algorithm for generic orbital basis sets, based on a non-convex sum-of-squares representation of the Hamiltonian and spectral-gap amplification. We compare it with the state-of-the-art second-quantised algorithm, compiling both with the same techniques at the logical level. For six molecules with 4 to 24 electrons in 5 to 14 times as many orbitals, our first-quantised method needs up to 16 times fewer Toffoli gates than the second-quantised algorithm in the low-qubit regime, where only a few hundred logical qubits are available. It stays cheaper up to between several hundred and about 1\,400 qubits, and its smallest qubit count is 1.4 to 2 times lower than in second quantisation. In the low-qubit and low-filling regime, our algorithm is therefore the method of choice for chemistry in orbital basis sets.

</details>


### [107] [CREST: A Hardware-Aware GNN-Based Framework for High-Fidelity and Robust Superconducting Quantum Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24886)
*Wenjie Sun,Xiaoyu Li,Zhigang Wang,Yuchen He,Shengbin Wang,Hantao Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于图神经网络的CREST框架，在路由前评估量子线路与候选执行区域的兼容性，从而提升超导处理器上的执行保真度并适应标定更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子编译需为输入线路选择连通的执行区域，该选择约束后续路由并影响噪声暴露；仅凭聚合硬件质量指标难以可靠预测特定线路的执行保真度，且标定更新会改变区域适用性并迫使重建依赖标定的资源结构。

Method: CREST以GNN编码线路的门依赖与逻辑比特交互，同时编码候选区域的连通结构与当前标定属性；通过双向跨图注意力关联线路与区域两种表示；以真实设备执行结果作为监督信号对候选区域排序；训练所得评分器可直接复用更新的标定属性，无需重训。

Result: 在TianYan-176上对来自7个数据集的324条线路评估，平均测量分布保真度较最强基线提升19.03%，并在每个数据集上取得最高平均保真度；使用更新标定属性时，同一模型在6个测量日期上无需重训仍保持最高总体平均保真度。

Conclusion: 统一的线路—区域兼容性模型使CREST在多样化工作负载上提升执行保真度，并在硬件标定变化中保持该优势。

Abstract: Quantum compilation on superconducting processors requires selecting a connected execution region whose topology and calibrated properties suit the input circuit. This choice constrains subsequent routing and influences the noise encountered during execution, yet aggregate hardware-quality metrics alone may not reliably predict the execution fidelity of a given circuit. Calibration updates can also change region suitability and trigger the rebuilding of calibration-dependent resource structures. We present CREST, a graph neural network (GNN)-based framework that evaluates circuit-region compatibility before routing. It encodes gate dependencies and logical-qubit interactions alongside each candidate region's connectivity and current calibration attributes; bidirectional cross-graph attention relates the two representations, while real-device execution outcomes supervise candidate ranking. The trained scorer is reused with updated calibration attributes. Evaluations on 324 circuits from seven datasets on TianYan-176 show that CREST improves mean measurement-distribution fidelity by 19.03% over the strongest baseline and achieves the highest mean fidelity on every dataset. Furthermore, using updated calibration attributes, the same trained model maintains the highest overall mean fidelity across six measurement dates without retraining. With a unified model of circuit-region compatibility, CREST improves execution fidelity across diverse workloads and sustains that advantage as hardware calibration changes.

</details>


### [108] [Residual-Algebraic Design of Symmetry-Preserving Variational Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24890)
*Sahinur Reja*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"残余对易子诊断"框架：受限SP生成集产生的残余动力学李代数可将目标对称扇区分解为不变块，块布居数守恒给出保真度上界；据此发展出在变分优化前筛选SP生成元的电路设计准则，并在XXZ与Hubbard模型中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称保持(SP)变分量子电路只把演化限制在物理相关子空间，但"对称保持"并不保证该子空间内的可及性(可达性)，即无法保证能到达任意目标态。需要一种可预先诊断这一可及性缺陷并指导电路设计的工具。

Method: 1) 指出受限生成集可具有非平凡的残余动力学李代数(DLA)，其对易子(residual commutant)进一步把对称扇区分解为不变块，块布居数守恒，从而给出与目标态保真度的运动学上界；2) 将该诊断反转为设计原则：在变分优化前筛选候选SP生成元，考察其是否破坏残余对易子、耦合哪些不变块、以及在残余代数下生成何种伴随模；3) 定理：对于两个正交残余块，单个基本实块间旋转即可生成完整正交代数；并给出复逃逸判据——若块间种子(seed)的实部与虚部在残余 intertwiner 代数上线性无关，则生成模为完整复耦合空间，扩展后代数升为特殊酉代数；4) 通过连通残余块图给出多块推广，并提示最小代价生成树式电路设计；5) 在XXZ与Hubbard模型中做实例演示。

Result: 理论上得到块布居守恒导致的保真度上界、实正交情形与复特殊酉情形的代数生成判据，以及基于残余块图的多块推广；实验上在XXZ与Hubbard模型中，残余代数筛选成功识别出能消除相关可及性障碍的SP电路扩展。

Conclusion: 对称保持本身不足以保证扇区内可达性；残余对易子分解与块布居守恒提供了定量诊断与保真度上界，而"是否破坏残余对易子/耦合哪些块/生成何种伴随模"构成可预测的电路设计准则，从而把残余对称性诊断、保真度界与量子电路设计统一为一条路线。

Abstract: Symmetry-preserving (SP) variational quantum circuits restrict evolution to physically relevant sectors of Hilbert space, but symmetry preservation alone does not guarantee accessibility within those sectors. We show that a restricted generator set can possess a residual dynamical Lie algebra whose commutant further decomposes the target symmetry sector into invariant blocks. The associated block populations are conserved and impose a kinematic upper bound on the fidelity with a target state. We then invert this diagnostic into a circuit-design principle: candidate SP generators can be screened before variational optimization by asking whether they break the residual commutant, which invariant blocks they couple, and what adjoint module they generate under the residual algebra. For two orthogonal residual blocks we prove that a single elementary real inter-block rotation generates the full orthogonal algebra. We further derive a complex escape criterion: if the real and imaginary parts of an inter-block seed are independent over the residual intertwiner algebra, the generated module is the full complex coupling space and the enlarged algebra becomes special unitary. A connected residual-block graph yields a natural multi-block generalization and suggests minimum-cost spanning-tree circuit design. We demonstrate this design principle in the XXZ and Hubbard models, where residual-algebra screening identifies SP circuit extensions that remove the relevant accessibility obstructions. These examples establish a unified route from residual-symmetry diagnosis and fidelity bounds to predictive quantum circuit design.

</details>


### [109] [Dressed-State Quantum Information Processing Using a Multilayer Ion Trap Chip with an Integrated Radio Frequency Resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24910)
*Ivan A. Boldin,Elham Esteki,Bogdan Okhrimenko,Friederike Giebel,Konstantin Thronberens,Amado Bautista-Salvador,Christian Ospelkaus,Christof Wunderlich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于多层微加工离子阱芯片及其集成RF谐振器产生的RF磁场梯度，实现射频缀饰态量子比特的制备、相干操作、选择性读出与双离子晶体中的独立寻址。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频缀饰态量子比特（RF-dressed-state qubit）在量子信息处理中具有鲁棒性等优势，但缺乏与之匹配、能够同时提供独立寻址与比特间耦合的专用阱芯片硬件。

Method: 设计、制造并表征专为缀饰态量子比特量子信息处理定制的多层离子阱芯片，其中一层集成RF谐振器，沿线形离子晶体轴向产生RF磁场幅度梯度；以激光冷却的¹⁷¹Yb⁺离子电子基态超精细态为对象，施加约12.6 GHz的单一全局调制RF驱动场激发缀饰态共振，进行实验表征。

Result: 实现缀饰态制备、单缀饰比特相干操作、缀饰态选择性读出，并在双离子库仑晶体中演示缀饰态量子比特的独立寻址。

Conclusion: 集成RF谐振器的多层阱芯片可同时提供缀饰比特的独立寻址与比特间耦合能力，为基于射频缀饰态的离子阱量子信息处理提供可行硬件平台。

Abstract: We present the experimental preparation of a radio frequency (RF)-dressed-state qubit, the coherent operation of a single RF-dressed qubit, and the selective readout of RF-dressed hyperfine states of $^{171}$Yb${^+}$ ions trapped using a novel microstructured ion trap chip. We demonstrate the individual addressing of RF-dressed-state qubits in a two-ion Coulomb crystal. To this end, we developed, manufactured, and characterized a trap chip specifcally designed for quantum information processing with RF-dressed-state qubits. The chip consists of multiple layers, one of which includes an integrated resonator that creates a gradient of the RF magnetic field amplitude along the axis of a linear ion crystal. This RF gradient enables the individual addressing of dressed qubit states as well as their coupling. We characterize the ion trap chip experimentally using the hyperfine states of the electronic ground state of laser cooled $^{171}$Yb${^+}$ ions and by applying a single, global, modulated RF field oscillating near 12.6 GHz that drives dressed-state resonances.
  We present a microfabricated multilayer ion trap chip specifically designed for quantum information processing based on dressed-state qubits. This ion trap chip consists of multiple layers, one of which includes an integrated radio frequency (RF) resonator, creating a gradient of the RF magnetic field amplitude that allows for individual qubit addressing and qubit-qubit coupling. We experimentally characterize this ion trap chip demonstrating dressed-qubit state preparation, coherent single dressed-qubit operation, and dressed-state-selective read-out using hyperfine states of the electronic ground state of $^{171}$Yb${^+}$. For this purpose, a global modulated RF driving field oscillating near 12.6 GHz is applied to the ions. In addition, we demonstrate individual addressing of dressed-state qubits in a two-ion Coulomb crystal.

</details>

### [110] [Critical--localized reentry under periodic spacer control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23796)
*Xin Wang,Pei-Jie Chang,Dong Ruan,Gui-Lu Long*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在匹配二聚体链中仅调节间隔位点能，证明在参考算符开带上物理谱为临界（纯奇异连续、零Lyapunov指数），带外为纯点谱，并验证同一固定谱分数上的临界—局域反复交替。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验准周期谱中"临界—局域在非零谱权重上反复交替"是否能对固定比例的谱被追踪到，而非仅停留于逐点或测度零的陈述。

Method: 利用精确的Bloch行列式恒等式，把物理转移判别式与周期参考算符的能带相联系；对黄金格频率，在相位全集测集与任意有限间隔控制下，证明带内临界性与带外纯点性；并用经过验证的IDS界证明固定谱分数在相继控制值下的反复交替。间隔位移使物理能级与参考带边以不同速率移动，用于重塑有限链回返窗口并确定端点；仿射重标定可精确平移整族控制参数。

Result: 参考能带内物理谱为纯奇异连续谱、零Lyapunov指数（临界）；带外为指数局域本征函数的纯点谱；IDS界给出同一固定谱分数在相继间隔控制下的临界—局域反复交替；可实现回返窗口端点定位与精确的仿射控制重标定。

Conclusion: 临界—局域交替可在正谱权重的固定谱分数上严格成立，且可由单一间隔参数的位移与仿射重标定加以设计和定位。

Abstract: Tracking a fixed fraction of a quasiperiodic spectrum tests whether repeated critical--localized alternation extends over a nonzero spectral weight. We find such alternation in a matched-dimer chain by varying only the spacer on-site energies and following a fixed interval of integrated-density-of-states (IDS) labels. An exact Bloch-determinant identity links the physical transfer discriminant to the bands of a periodic reference operator. For the golden cell frequency, we prove that, on a common full-measure set of phases and for every finite value of the spacer control, the physical spectrum in the open reference bands is critical, with purely singular-continuous spectrum and zero Lyapunov exponent, while the spectrum strictly outside the bands is pure point, with exponentially decaying eigenfunctions. Validated IDS bounds certify repeated critical--localized alternation of the same fixed spectral fraction at successive spacer controls. Spacer shifts move the physical levels and reference-band edges at different rates, allowing us to reshape finite-chain return windows and place their endpoints at prescribed controls. An affine recalibration relocates the control family exactly.

</details>


### [111] [Wegner model in high dimension: singular continuous spectrum from U(1) symmetry breaking and AAT self-consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24515)
*Julian Arenz,Martin R. Zirnbauer*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文利用场论与统计力学方法分析高维Wegner单轨道模型的能谱分解，发现在Anderson转变点处存在奇异连续型局域谱测度，其机制是场论整体对称群非紧性导致的新型自发对称破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为与奇异连续谱相关的无序现象提供场论基础，并厘清Anderson转变点处能谱的类型。

Method: 对高维Wegner单轨道模型作能谱分解，借助场论与统计力学的对称性分析；用Abou-Chacra–Anderson–Thouless自洽局域化理论近似描述对称破缺的极值态。

Result: 在Anderson转变处识别出奇异连续型的局域谱测度；金属相中非紧整体对称群破缺到紧子群，而临界点处则自发破缺到幂零子群。

Conclusion: 非紧对称群的自发对称破缺是奇异连续谱产生的关键机制，为无序系统中的奇异连续谱现象建立了场论框架。

Abstract: We analyze the energy-spectral decomposition of the Wegner one-orbital model in high lattice dimension using ideas from field theory and statistical mechanics. Most notably, we identify a local spectral measure of singular continuous type for the disordered model at the Anderson transition. The key mechanism that leads to a singular continuous spectrum is a novel scenario of spontaneous symmetry breaking, intimately connected to the non-compactness of the global symmetry group of the field theory. While that symmetry breaks down to a compact subgroup in the metallic phase, it spontaneously breaks down to a nilpotent subgroup at criticality. We describe the symmetry-breaking extremal states approximately, by applying the self-consistent theory of localization due to Abou-Chacra, Anderson, and Thouless. The thrust of our work is to provide a field-theoretical foundation for disorder phenomena associated with singular continuous spectra.

</details>
