### [112] [Topological signatures of criticality: the Ising case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23586)
*Tak-Shing Chan,Paweł Dłotko,Jacek Gulgowski,Biagio Lucini,Mateusz Masłowski,Davide Vadacchino*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在二维方格与三角格点Ising模型中，将正负自旋域关联为胞腔复形并以二者欧拉示性数之差构造拓扑观测量，证明其可表为常规观测量组合、给出有限温度下欧拉示性数期望的正下界（否定既有猜想），并通过Monte Carlo与多直方图重加权验证其类序参量行为、有限尺寸标度可定位临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观构型的拓扑性质可能提供超越常规热力学观测量的相变信息；作者希望检验这一设想，并探索在无法构造序参量的情形下，用纯几何定义的拓扑量定位有序—无序相变的可能性。

Method: 在二维方格与三角格点Ising模型中，把正、负自旋畴分别关联到胞腔复形（cell complex），以其欧拉示性数之差作为"自旋奇"拓扑观测量；解析推导该量与常规Ising观测量（磁化、能量等）的关系式以获得物理预期；用Monte Carlo模拟结合多直方图重加权，考察其在铁磁转变附近的序参量式行为及磁化率有限尺寸标度；并对幂律拟合是否加入次领头修正项作对比。

Result: 该拓扑观测量在铁磁转变附近呈序参量式行为；其磁化率有限尺寸标度给出的赝临界温度在两种格子上均收敛到已知临界温度。方格上由拓扑量得到的临界指数领头估计对拟合区间比磁化估计更敏感，加入额外修正项的拟合支持这一差异源于更强的次领头有限尺寸修正；三角格上相应估计与Ising理论值一致。此外证明任意有限正温度下欧拉示性数期望超过一个与格点尺寸无关的正下界，从而否定先前提出的一个猜想。

Conclusion: 拓扑定义的量（如欧拉示性数）可作为定位有序—无序相变的替代途径；由于其定义纯属几何、不依赖序参量的构造，此类观测量的思路有望推广到难以定义常规序参量的系统。

Abstract: Topological properties of microscopic configurations may provide additional insight into phase transitions beyond conventional thermodynamic observables. We investigate this idea in the two-dimensional Ising model on square and triangular lattices by associating cell complexes with the positive and negative spin domains and constructing an Euler-characteristic observable from their difference. We provide the expression relating this quantity to conventional observables of the Ising model. This derivation allows us to understand its expected behavior physically. As a byproduct, we show that at any finite positive temperature the expectation of the Euler characteristic exceeds a positive, lattice-size-independent bound, hence disproving a previously suggested conjecture. Monte Carlo simulations combined with multihistogram reweighting show that this spin-odd topological observable exhibits order-parameter-like behavior near the ferromagnetic transition. Finite-size scaling of its susceptibility yields pseudocritical temperatures that converge to the known critical temperature for both lattice geometries. On the square lattice, the leading-order estimate of the critical exponent obtained from the topological observable depends more strongly on the fitting range than the magnetization estimate. This behavior is consistent with stronger subleading finite-size corrections, as supported by fits including an additional correction term. On the triangular lattice, the corresponding estimate remains consistent with the theoretical Ising value. Our study suggests that topologically defined quantities such as the Euler characteristic can provide an alternative way to locate order-disorder phase transitions in the systems considered here. Since the definition of these observables is purely geometrical, they can provide useful insights in cases in which order parameters cannot be constructed.

</details>


### [113] [Persistent Hydration and Ionic Mobility: When the Ion Is Not the Only Transported Species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23605)
*Biman Bagchi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出将离子、第一水合壳层与外层溶剂视为动态耦合组分的分子框架，用平均力势与配位动力学描述局域水合结构与交换，用分子记忆函数描述溶剂集体响应，解释了为何水合效应不能被简化为加和摩擦或单一放大半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小离子（尤其Li+、Mg2+、Ca2+等强水合离子）在水中输运的理论理解仍不完整，其第一水合壳层寿命可与平动运动时间尺度相当甚至更长，连续介质模型与常规分子描述均难以准确刻画。

Method: 构建离子—第一水合壳层—外层溶剂三相动态耦合的分子理论框架：以平均力势（PMF）与配位动力学表征局域水合结构与配体交换，以分子记忆函数（广义朗之万/记忆摩擦核）表征溶剂的集体响应。

Result: 该理论表明水合对离子输运的影响本质上是非加和的、且无法用单一等效放大半径等价替代，从而揭示传统连续介质与简化分子模型失效的根源。

Conclusion: 该框架为超越常规连续介质描述与简化分子模型提供了统一途径，可用于定量刻画强水合小离子的输运行为。

Abstract: Small-ion transport in water remains only partially understood despite more than a century of theoretical work. The difficulty is especially acute for strongly hydrated ions such as Li$^{+}$, Mg$^{2+}$, and Ca$^{2+}$, whose first hydration shells can persist on timescales comparable to or longer than translational motion. We develop a molecular framework in which the ion, its first hydration shell, and the outer solvent are treated as dynamically coupled components. Local hydration structure and exchange are represented through a potential of mean force and coordination dynamics, while collective solvent response is described through molecular memory functions. The theory clarifies why hydration cannot generally be represented by an additive friction or a single enlarged radius, and provides a route beyond conventional continuum and molecular descriptions.

</details>


### [114] [Finite-time stochastic resetting in bounded domains: competing targets, energetic cost, and optimal search strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23790)
*Hanshuang Chen,Chongyao Tang,Guofeng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究有界一维区间内、双吸收边界条件下布朗粒子在有限时间随机重置下的首达动力学与能量代价，解析求解平均全局首达时间、分裂概率与总功生成函数，并给出平均总功与累积返回时间之间的精确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机重置研究多假设瞬时返回，而实际重置过程需要有限时间。本文旨在刻画有限时长返回过程（由线性束缚势驱动）如何改变重置对搜索过程的加速效果，并量化其能量开销与效率权衡。

Method: 在具两个吸收边界的有界一维域内，将粒子以有限时长、由线性束缚势驱动的过程拉回指定重置位置。通过求解相应的后向方程，得到平均全局首达时间与分裂概率的解析表达式；通过推导总功的生成函数获得平均总功的解析式，并建立平均总功与平均累积返回时间之间的精确关系，最后用布朗动力学模拟验证。

Result: 有限返回时长显著改变平均全局首达时间对重置率的依赖；存在一个临界束缚强度，超过该值重置才能加速搜索；并刻画了有利重置位置范围随束缚强度的变化。对线性束缚势，平均总功与平均累积返回时间满足精确关系，据此给出首达时间与能量消耗之间的定量权衡，揭示返回时长对搜索效率的影响。模拟结果与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 有限时间重置并非瞬时重置的简单近似：返回过程的时长与束缚强度共同决定重置能否加速搜索及有利重置位置区间，同时决定能量代价；平均总功与累积返回时间的精确关系为搜索效率与能耗的权衡提供了定量框架。

Abstract: We investigate the first-passage dynamics and energetic cost of a Brownian particle subject to finite-time stochastic resetting in a bounded one-dimensional domain with two absorbing boundaries. Unlike conventional instantaneous resetting, the particle is returned to a prescribed resetting position through a finite-duration process driven by a linear trapping potential. By solving the corresponding backward equations, we derive analytical expressions for the mean global first-passage time and the splitting probabilities. Our results show that the finite duration of the return process can substantially modify the dependence of the mean global first-passage time on the resetting rate. In particular, we identify a critical trapping strength above which resetting can accelerate the search process, and characterize how the range of favorable resetting positions depends on the trapping strength. We further characterize the energetic cost of resetting by deriving the generating function of the total work and obtaining an analytical expression for its mean value. For the linear trapping potential considered here, we establish an exact relation between the mean total work and the mean cumulative return time. This relation allows us to establish a quantitative trade-off between the mean global first-passage time and the energetic expenditure, revealing how the finite duration of the return process affects search efficiency. Brownian dynamics simulations confirm our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [115] [Blanket theory for dense networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23960)
*Simone Franchini*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文利用Blanket理论给出稠密自旋网络中cavity泛函的构造性计算方法，从基态附近的低温涨落与稀疏缺陷出发，揭示线性能量的REM普适性与分层重组机制，将cavity场与Gibbs权重识别为Aizenman-Sims-Starr随机重叠结构，从而导出显式自由能泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为稠密自旋网络（含非高斯耦合、分层结构如受限/深度玻尔兹曼机）提供一个统一的、构造性的cavity泛函计算方法，并解释REM普适性与自由能中Parisi型分层结构的来源。

Method: 以Blanket理论的界面与磁化壳层描述为基础，在低温区从基态涨落出发，利用稀疏缺陷的行为展示线性能量的REM普适性；缺陷在各层间的共享与更新使能量和Gibbs权重形成层级结构；由于同一组态决定新cavity场的协方差，据此把场与权重对应为Aizenman-Sims-Starr随机重叠结构，再用递归分数均值（fractional means）评估cavity贡献，得到由层磁化与层级指数参数化、系数由相互作用方差与网络归一化确定的显式自由能泛函；在给定矩与归一化条件下通过压力比较将极限平均压力从高斯代表推广到具有相同方差的独立中心化非高斯耦合网络。

Result: 得到显式自由能泛函并证明非高斯耦合下的极限平均压力与高斯情形一致；以确定性Curie-Weiss模型示例方法，对Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型恢复Parisi泛函，并导出受限玻尔兹曼机与深度玻尔兹曼机的泛函（含fullRSB极限）。

Conclusion: Blanket理论为稠密自旋网络的cavity泛函提供了统一构造框架，将REM普适性与Aizenman-Sims-Starr随机重叠结构结合，统一涵盖Curie-Weiss、SK及（深度）玻尔兹曼机等模型及其fullRSB极限。

Abstract: We show how Blanket theory provides a constructive method for calculating cavity functionals in dense spin networks. Building on the description through interfaces and magnetization shells, we develop the calculation from the low-temperature fluctuations around the ground state. In this regime, dilute defects reveal the mechanism of random energy model (REM) universality for linear energies, while their sharing and renewal across layers organize the energies and Gibbs weights into a hierarchy. Because the same configurations determine the covariances of the fresh cavity fields, this construction identifies the fields and weights with an Aizenman-Sims-Starr random overlap structure. The cavity contributions can then be evaluated through recursive fractional means, yielding an explicit free-energy functional parametrized by layer magnetizations and hierarchical exponents, with coefficients determined by the interaction variances and the network normalization. Under the stated moment and normalization conditions, comparison of pressures transfers the limiting mean pressure from the Gaussian representative to networks with independent centered non-Gaussian couplings having the same variances. We illustrate the method with the deterministic Curie-Weiss model, recover the Parisi functional for the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model, and derive the functionals for restricted and deep Boltzmann machines, including the full replica symmetry breaking (fullRSB) limit.

</details>


### [116] [Geometric Decomposition of Entropy Production Based on General $f$-Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24024)
*Rihito Nagase,Shoki Sugimoto,Takahiro Sagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文基于 f-散度，为过阻尼Langevin系统构建了一类普遍的熵产生"过量-维持"(excess-housekeeping)分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hatano-Sasa与Maes-Netočný两类经典分解长期并存，其弛豫性与最优传输性质缺乏统一结构框架。

Method: 以f-散度为工具定义过量熵产生率，令其等于f-散度趋向某弛豫目标的弛豫速率，并给出其最优传输表示；同时构造保持同一弛豫目标的维持速度场。

Result: 证明了过量熵产生率兼具f-散度弛豫速率与最优传输双重刻画；框架可导出Hatano-Sasa与Maes-Netočný分解为其特例。选取任意目标分布作为弛豫目标，可得到二者之外具有物理意义的新分解。

Conclusion: f-散度框架统一了两类分解的弛豫与最优传输面向，并将分解推广为由目标分布参数化的族。

Abstract: We construct a general class of excess--housekeeping decompositions of entropy production based on $f$-divergences for overdamped Langevin systems. The excess entropy production rate equals the relaxation rate of the $f$-divergence toward a relaxation target and also admits an optimal-transport representation, while the housekeeping velocity preserves the same relaxation target. Our framework recovers the Hatano--Sasa and Maes--Neto{č}n{ý} decompositions as special cases, and unifies their relaxational and optimal-transport aspects within a common structure. Moreover, our framework yields physically meaningful decompositions beyond these two by taking a desired distribution as the relaxation target.

</details>


### [117] [Neither graphs nor hypergraphs: structural moments of higher-order interactions determine stochastic social-impact dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24252)
*Emanuele Cozzo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究高阶交互何时可被约化为更简单的结构表征，提出"结构矩"（size-weighted inclusion moments）层级：对q次多项式的响应函数，动力学仅由至多涉及q个邻居的加权包含矩决定，且该约化是充分且必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶（群体）交互是否可约化为成对等更简单表示，通常只从结构层面讨论；作者主张这一问题本质取决于交互所驱动的动力学——"动力学看不到的即可丢弃"，并希望对此给出精确刻画。

Method: 针对二元状态随机动力学（广义投票者模型、社会影响模型在超图上的推广）：每个个体被更新时与随机选取的群体交互，并按意见不一致成员的比例改变状态。作者分析哪些群体交互过程的统计量决定所得的马尔可夫动力学，界定不同规则类对应的最小结构描述。

Result: 对q次多项式响应函数，决定动力学的是规模加权的包含矩（结构矩）层级，最多涉及q个邻居，与群体规模无关；该条件既充分又必要——两种交互结构对所有此类规则产生相同动力学，当且仅当其矩相等。不同规则类对应不同最小描述：线性规则退化为加权成对连边，任意规则则需完整的按群体规模的共同成员分布。对投票者型规则，这些矩还能重构动力学的精确成对复制表示。

Conclusion: 高阶交互的可约性由动力学决定而非仅由结构决定；存在由规则复杂度（多项式次数q）参数化的最小结构描述层级，为超图交互的降维与等价性判定提供了精确判据。

Abstract: Whether higher-order interactions can be reduced to simpler representations is a question about the dynamics they drive, not only about their structure: what can be discarded is whatever the dynamics does not see. We make this precise for binary-state stochastic dynamics in which each agent, when updated, interacts with a randomly selected group and changes state according to the fraction of disagreeing members, as in generalized voter and social-impact models on hypergraphs. We ask which statistics of the group-interaction process determine the resulting Markov dynamics. For response functions that are polynomials of degree $q$, the answer is a hierarchy of size-weighted inclusion moments, which we call structural moments, involving at most $q$ neighbors at a time, regardless of how large the groups are. These moments are not only sufficient but also necessary: two interaction structures produce identical dynamics for all such rules if and only if their moments coincide. More generally, each class of rules selects its own minimal structural description, ranging from weighted pairwise links for linear rules to the full distribution of co-membership by group size for arbitrary rules. For voter-type rules, the same moments also reconstruct exact pairwise copying representations of these dynamics.

</details>


### [118] [Phase transitions in work fluctuations in correlated N-particle resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24318)
*Felix Herber,Deepak Gupta,Supriya Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了N个同时受外部势切换（随机或周期）驱动的粒子系统，从任意关联初态出发，解析给出功分布在全参数空间（N、初始粒子间关联、初始单粒子约化分布）中的形式，发现参数空间被非平凡地、依赖N地划分为不同"相"，功的方差函数形式在相边界处不连续改变，相数取决于随机性来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作关注N个独立不相关粒子在相同环境随机变化下 dynamical 涌现的粒子间位置关联现象，但创建这些关联的热力学代价及其与初始分布的相互作用尚未被研究。

Method: 考虑N个粒子同时经历外部势切换（随机或周期性），初态可为任意关联态；在包含N、任意初始粒子间关联及任意初始单粒子约化分布的完整参数空间中，解析/系统地刻画热力学功的分布（尤其其方差）。

Result: 参数空间被非平凡且依赖N地划分为若干不同"相"，功方差的函数形式在相之间发生不连续变化；相的数目由动力学中随机性的来源决定。

Conclusion: 粒子间关联的建立具有可表征的热力学代价结构，其定性行为随参数区域呈"相变式"转变，为理解涌现关联的代价提供了相图式的分类框架。

Abstract: Systems of $N$ independent uncorrelated particles subjected to the same stochastic change in the environment have recently been studied both theoretically and in experiments, to elucidate the interesting phenomenon of dynamically emergent interparticle positional correlations. However, the thermodynamic cost of creating such correlations, as well as its interplay with the initial distribution of the $N$ particles has not been studied. In this paper, we study systems of $N$ particles that are simultaneously subjected to a switch of an external potential, either stochastically or periodically, beginning from arbitrarily correlated initial states. We map out the distribution of thermodynamic work for such a system in the full parameter space of the problem that includes the number of particles $N$, arbitrary initial correlations between particles and arbitrary initial reduced single particle distributions. We demonstrate that the parameter space is partitioned non-trivially, and in an $N$-dependent way, into distinct "phases", whereby the functional form of the variance of the work changes discontinuously across phases. The number of such phases depends on the source of stochasticity in the dynamics.

</details>


### [119] [Numerical integration of non-local master equations for renewal shot noise with drift and state-dependent amplitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24390)
*Marco Bianucci,Riccardo Mannella*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种高精度数值方法，求解状态依赖随机踢（非局部空间）与非指数等待时间（非局部时间）驱动系统的广义主方程，并用精确解与1亿轨迹蒙特卡洛验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 踢算子为无限阶导致空间非局部，非指数等待时间导致含漂移传播子的时间记忆核，且解包含塌缩到漂移吸引子的准奇异"从未被踢质量"分量；此类方程缺乏可靠数值方案，且需评估局部/泊松近似的误差。

Method: 踢算子视为沿Marcus流的推前映射，在Lamperti变量下化为平移，借助踢的累积分布精确计算格间权重（免积分）；漂移采用时间精确的守恒半拉格朗日步；记忆核用指数和表示，辅助场由漂移平流，代价随步数线性；从未被踢质量精确输运。逐项量化简化替代方案的误差。

Result: 与精确解及带精确流映射的事件驱动蒙特卡洛在10^8轨迹下统计不可区分；高斯踢调好的参数可直接用于其他踢分布；在耦合零点附近密度以快于任意幂次衰减，解遵循已知渐近律至10^-37量级，远超蒙特卡洛能力；相较局部方程偏差可达密度宽度的8%，即便等待时间均值有限，与误差估计一致。

Conclusion: 该方法是求解非局部时空主方程的精确高效方案，并提供Fortran实现；局部/泊松近似在慢漂移长时下存在可测且可预估的偏差，需谨慎使用。

Abstract: The probability density of a system driven by a drift and a renewal train of kicks with state-dependent amplitude obeys a master equation that is non-local in space, because the kick operator $\hat{p}(i\partial_x I(x))$ is of infinite order, and, for non-exponential waiting times, non-local in time, with a memory kernel containing the propagator of the drift. Its solutions contain quasi-singular components, the never-kicked mass, which collapse onto the attractors of the drift. We present an accurate numerical method for such equations. The kick operator is a push-forward along the Marcus flow; in the Lamperti variable it becomes a translation, whose exact cell-to-cell weights follow from the cumulative distribution of the kicks, without quadrature. The drift is integrated by a conservative semi-Lagrangian step exact in time. The memory is represented by a sum of exponentials whose auxiliary fields are advected by the drift, at a cost linear in the number of steps. The never-kicked mass is transported exactly. For each ingredient we quantify the errors of simpler alternatives. The method is verified against exact solutions and event-driven Monte Carlo simulations with exact flow maps, from which it is statistically indistinguishable with $10^8$ trajectories. Parameters tuned for Gaussian kicks carry over unchanged to other kick distributions, and near a zero of the coupling, where the density vanishes faster than any power, the solution follows the known asymptotic law down to densities of order $10^{-37}$, far beyond Monte Carlo reach. As an application we compare the exact equation with its local and Poissonian approximations: in the range explored, the local equation deviates by up to 8% of the density width, also for waiting times with finite mean, consistently with an error estimate predicting larger deviations for slow drifts at long times. A Fortran implementation is available.

</details>


### [120] [A Monte Carlo study of closely packed two-patch particles with frustrated interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24560)
*Rui Ding,Yan-Wei Li,Hao Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在密堆二维两补丁粒子体系中引入排斥-排斥、吸引-非补丁、中等非补丁相互作用，产生几何阻挫，基态为取向无序且关联函数指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期研究发现密堆两补丁粒子具有丰富的取向有序行为（非单调的向列序参量变化、双 BKT 相变），本文欲探究在该体系中引入相互竞争相互作用后是否可产生阻挫并导致取向无序基态。

Method: 设定补丁-补丁排斥、补丁-非补丁吸引、非补丁-非补丁中等相互作用的规则；设计带单缺陷传播（single-defect propagation）的马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗（MCMC）算法以搜索基态。

Result: 模拟显示基态为无序态，关联函数随距离呈指数衰减。

Conclusion: 二维密堆体系中可通过设计补丁粒子来构造并探索取向无序系统。

Abstract: Recently it was found that closely packed two-patch particles exhibit rich orientational ordering behaviors, e.g., nonmonotonic changes of the nematic order parameter and double BKT phase transitions [arXiv:2509.03981]. In this work, we show that, with repulsive patch-patch, attractive patch-nonpatch and intermediate nonpatch-nonpatch interactions, there exist frustrations in the system of closely packed two-patch particles. To investigate the ground states of the system, we design a Markov Chain Monte Carlo algorithm with single-defect propagation. The simulation results show that the ground states are disordered and the correlation functions decay exponentially with the distance. These results demonstrate that in 2D close packing one could design patchy particles to explore orientationally disordered systems.

</details>


### [121] [When Time-Reversible Sensor Records Require Hidden Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24898)
*Andrey V. Dobrynin,Anastasia Stroujkova*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究二值（二元）传感器记录的可逆性与其在有限状态马尔可夫模型中必然耗散之间的矛盾，给出了精确的平衡判据与与隐状态数无关的熵产生正下界，并给出最小耗散的上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间可逆（细致平衡）的观测统计并不蕴含底层机制无耗散：同一二值观测记录可能在任何兼容的有限状态马尔可夫模型中都需要耗散，因此需要建立仅凭可观测统计判断平衡与否、并量化最小耗散的判据。

Method: 以四状态传感器（具有相关驻留时间）为模型，利用两条可观测的开关（switching）统计量构造精确的平衡判据；在排除平衡后，由该统计量导出熵产生的正下界（不依赖隐状态数目）；再通过显式的四状态构造给出最小耗散的上界；最后对含噪泊松传感器比较上下界。

Result: 两条可观测开关统计给出精确平衡判据；排除平衡时得到与隐状态数无关的熵产生正下界；显式四状态构造给出最小耗散上界；对含噪泊松传感器，上下界相差不到两倍，并在某个动力学匹配点同时趋于零，而传感器本身仍保持耗散性。

Conclusion: 仅凭二值观测统计即可判定平衡并给出熵产生的不依赖隐状态数的界，且界在显式构造下接近紧致（<2 倍差距）；但界在动力学匹配点的消失只是界本身的松紧变化，并不代表真实耗散为零，说明识别真实耗散需更精细的可观测量。

Abstract: A binary sensor record can be time reversible yet require dissipation in every compatible finite-state Markov model. For a four-state sensor with correlated dwell times, two observed switching statistics give an exact equilibrium criterion and, when equilibrium is excluded, a positive lower bound on entropy production independent of hidden-state number. An explicit four-state construction provides an upper bound on the minimum dissipation. For a noisy Poisson sensor, the bounds differ by less than a factor of two and vanish at a kinetic matching point while the sensor remains dissipative.

</details>
